## Review execution

Cycle 1 reviewed plans at commit `fd3e346`. Configured defaults were preserved: Fable (`claude-fable-5-1`), Grok (`openrouter/x-ai/grok-4.7`), Gemini (`openrouter/google/gemini-4-argon`). No reviewer flags overrode selection; repository configuration was not modified.

- **Fable:** complete source-cited review, all nine plans covered, first attempt successful.
- **Grok:** first 660-second attempt timed out after an intent-only response (all nine plan sections missing). One corrected bounded 1200-second retry completed with a source-cited review covering all nine plans. The earlier intent-only response is preserved below and contributes no findings.
- **Gemini:** first 660-second attempt timed out without a final review; bounded retry failed with OpenCode `database is locked` / EPIPE. Diagnostic stubs are preserved, and no Gemini approval or consensus is inferred.
- A brief retry launch from a temporary config directory was cancelled after discovering that the runner inherits process cwd; the corrected retry used `--cwd` for config resolution while process cwd remained the source checkout. No content from the cancelled attempt is counted.

Configured default reviewers are preferences; explicit reviewer flags would be assertions. The workflow allows a report when at least one real review succeeds. Gemini is a detected adapter instance that failed operationally, not a missing binary. Any future-cycle omission must disclose that temporary unavailability and retain these failure records; configuration stays unchanged.

Diagnostics (outputs only, not duplicate prompt/plan inputs):

- Initial run: `.planning/phases/06.5-remaining-presentation-contracts-and-decided-cleanup/.review-diagnostics/20261102-cycle1-gsd-review-_2_i7709`.

- Complete cited-symbol/path/flag inventory, with every plan occurrence and exact line quote: initial diagnostics `source-grounding.json`.

## Claude Review (fable-review)

I verified the plans against the checkout. Every file the plans cite exists at the stated seams, all three cached interpreters and the vitest/mkdocs/ruff entry points are present, and the research's defect claims (title-slug IDs, no active panel, standalone modal icon, alias accepted with False) reproduce in source. The review below is organized per plan as required; `file:line` references are to the current working tree.

## 06.5-01

**Summary.** The plan correctly targets the only two producers (`CvHeadingNode.render` at `src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:339-353` and the layered table in `src/crud_views/lib/button_classes.py:135-155`) and the one stock modal site (`src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/modal/content.html:3`). The size/level split is sound and default bytes can stay identical. Two mechanics are unsettled, and one existing guard test will fail as written.

**Strengths**
- The size rule matches current behavior exactly: today `classes = [f"h{self.level}"] if rendered != self.level else []` (`crud_views.py:348`), so "implicit size only on level change" preserves every golden in `tests/test1/golden/heading_level/`.
- Correct reading of the registry: `get_button_class` uses presence not truthiness (`button_classes.py:151-153`), and host validation already keys on `:icon-host` with `value.format(name_class="x")` (`button_classes.py:258-262`), so the new `heading*:icon-host` keys get E363 for free.
- The plan knows W362 only accepts `key in DEFAULT_BUTTON_CLASSES or _is_action_mode_key(key)` (`button_classes.py:300-302`) and explicitly extends recognition for the optional location keys without prefilling `None` defaults.
- Host-vs-element split reuses `resolve_icon_name_class` (`src/crud_views/lib/icons.py:248-267`), the same detector `icon_host_keys` uses for buttons (`src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:99-108`), so a disabled host slot cannot fall back to an inline duplicate.

**Concerns**
- **HIGH: the KEYS-04 guard will fail in this plan's own verify.** `tests/test1/test_button_classes.py:95` asserts `len(EXPECTED_KEYS) == 74` and `:102-106` parses "The default table has 74 keys" from `docs/reference/buttons.md:147`. Adding six `heading:size-N` plus shared/location icon keys to `DEFAULT_BUTTON_CLASSES` breaks both. `docs/reference/buttons.md` is not in plan 01's or plan 02's `files_modified`, yet task 01-02's verify selects `-k 'button_classes'`. Plan 07-03 touches `buttons.md` only for the alias.
- **MEDIUM: no class-composition mechanism for host classes.** `cv_heading` accepts exactly one `class=` expression (`crud_views.py:370-373`). The action says "attach host_class to the actual heading alongside modal-title" but the only template-level composition is `|add`, and `""|add:" modal-title"` yields a leading space, so `class=" modal-title"` and the `modal_detail.html` golden (`tests/test1/test_heading_level.py:95-99`, `:165`) would change. Acceptance "size omission preserves existing tag bytes" is unachievable without defining this.
- **LOW:** "explicit size always resolves the key" produces `<h3 class="h3">` when rendered level equals size. Harmless, but the contract should say so, or suppress when equal.
- **LOW:** the modal template has a literal space between `{% cv_header_icon %}` and `{% cv_header %}` (`modal/content.html:3`); the migrated template must keep it or the modal golden drifts for element icons.

**Suggestions**
- Add `docs/reference/buttons.md` and the `EXPECTED_KEYS` update to plan 01-02's files, and quote the new count.
- Give `cv_heading` an optional `icon=` kwarg taking the `HeadingIconPresentation`: append `host_class` to the class list and emit `inline_html` before the body. One tag, no string concatenation in templates, and the `class=""` edge case stays byte-identical.
- State that `_heading_class` reads `_settings_layer()` uncached (or hooks the same `setting_changed` reset at `button_classes.py:120-122`), since the merged `_button_classes_cache` cannot answer layer-first questions.

**Risk: MEDIUM.** Design is right; the plan will not pass its own verify until the key-count guard and class composition are addressed.

## 06.5-02

**Summary.** Migrates the two host pages and the two plain icon-bearing group layouts, and publishes an executed recipe. The site inventory is accurate: the test host nests `<h3>` inside `<h5 class="d-flex">` (`tests/test1/app/templates/app/crud_views.html:14-27`), the demo hardcodes `class="h3 mb-0"` (`examples/bootstrap5/project/templates/project/crud_views.html:17`), and split-card/striped-rows render the icon as a sibling outside the heading (`layouts/split-card/group.html:8-9`, `layouts/striped-rows/group.html:4-5`). The proposed golden exclusion rule is the weak point.

**Strengths**
- No golden under `tests/test1/golden/` captures the host wrapper (grep for the flex wrapper or `<h3>` returned nothing), so rewriting the test host does not cascade into unrelated goldens.
- `tests/test1/od/test_icons.py:36-45` asserts `'<i class="truck'`; `render_icon("truck", css="me-2")` still yields `class="truck me-2"`, so moving icons to the shared inline slot keeps those assertions green.
- The executable-docs pattern (`tests/test1/test_conditional_migration_docs.py:31-38`) is the right analog for `test_heading_docs.py`.

**Concerns**
- **MEDIUM: "card-header ancestor" exclusion is ambiguous.** `test_heading_level.py:27` currently excludes `ancestor-or-self::h5[contains(@class,'d-flex')]`. The replacement "page card-header ancestor" collides with `layouts/card-rows/group.html:3-4` and `layouts/striped-rows/group.html:3-5`, which put their `cv_heading` inside `<div class="card-header">`. `od_card-rows` / `od_striped-rows` goldens are built via `_render_group` and would be emptied, tripping `assert actual` at `:97`.
- **LOW:** `tests/test1/test_manage.py:273` selects `h3, h4` on a page that may extend the host; a `<h1 class="h3">` title changes that list. Add to read_first.
- **LOW:** split-card today styles its icon `text-default fs-5 me-2` (`split-card/group.html:8`); the shared default `me-2` drops size/color. The plan says to record it, but it must land as an intentional-markup entry in plan 09's CHANGELOG, not just a SUMMARY note.
- **LOW:** `tests/test1/od/test_icons.py` is affected by 02-02 and 03-01 but owned by no plan.

**Suggestions**
- Mark the host title with a host-specific attribute (e.g. `data-cv-host-title`) and exclude on that, not on a Bootstrap class.
- Add `test_manage.py` and `od/test_icons.py` to read_first.

**Risk: MEDIUM.** Scope is right; the exclusion rule needs a precise selector.

## 06.5-03

**Summary.** The strongest plan. It fixes the two real defects (`forloop.first` on the trigger but `tab-pane fade` without `active` in `layouts/tabs-vertical/group.html:2`, and title-slug IDs plus fixed `objectDetailAccordion` in `accordion/object_detail.html:2`) by projecting identity once in `render_object_detail`, which today renders a fresh context with only `groups`/`layout` (`templatetags/crud_views_object_detail.py:38`).

**Strengths**
- The encoding is injective. After `cvod-c{n}-{token}` the next segment is one of `-s{m}-`, `-i{d}`, `-root`, `-tabs`, `-content`, and the token grammar `[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]*` cannot produce a `-s`/`-i` ambiguity because lengths are explicit. All characters are selector-safe for `data-bs-target="#..."`.
- Explicitly keeps `group`/`prop` in context, so copied layouts and `test_layout_packs.py:48-70`, `test_layout_a11y.py` keep working, and `render_group(..., presentation=None, component=None)` is backward compatible with `{% render_group group layout=layout %}` callers (`object_detail.html:3`, `test_heading_level.py:46`).
- Standalone `render_group` gets a local projection and omits `data-bs-parent`, closing a real gap in the accordion template (`accordion/group.html:14-16`).
- Golden normalization is bounded to generated attribute values; `od_accordion.html` is the only heading golden carrying a target.

**Concerns**
- **MEDIUM:** collision registry is introduced in 03-02 ("render-local registry ... explicitly carry into nested fresh contexts") and redesigned in 04-02 ("own on the earliest non-base render-context frame"). Two implementations of one mechanism across waves invite rework; 03-02 should implement the explicit-transport half and leave frame ownership entirely to 04.
- **LOW:** `tabindex=0` on every panel is the APG recommendation only when the panel has no focusable content; say that in the contract so themes do not treat it as mandatory.
- **LOW:** the panel still renders a visible `cv_heading level=5` with the title (`tabs-vertical/group.html:6`) and `test_heading_level.py:108` depends on its `mb-1` class; the plan should state that this heading is unchanged.

**Suggestions**
- Name the standalone tab panel's `aria-label` source (escaped `group.title`) in acceptance.
- Add `od/test_icons.py` to files, since `test_group_icon_tabs_vertical*` exercise the migrated button.

**Risk: LOW-MEDIUM.**

## 06.5-04

**Summary.** Adds stable supplied identity and bounds collision detection to one outer render. I traced the Django mechanics the plan relies on: `Template.render` pushes a render-context frame, `ExtendsNode` uses `_render` without pushing, `IncludeNode` pushes a new frame on the same `RenderContext`, and the backend `tpl.render(dict, request)` used by the tags creates a new `Context`. So "earliest non-base frame" plus explicit transport into the fresh backend contexts is a workable boundary.

**Strengths**
- Three-series verify is mandatory, which is right for an approach touching `render_context.dicts`.
- `mixins.py:44-51` is the correct threading point and the plan keeps `self.object`; `parse_property_display` at `config.py:88` makes `id` a pure additive key.
- The page/modal namespace obligation is placed on the application, matching D-05/D-07.

**Concerns**
- **MEDIUM: config errors surface only at request time.** Dict groups are validated by E242–E245 at check time (`mixins.py:72-110`), but an invalid or duplicate supplied `id` would raise from `PropertyGroupConfig` inside `get_property_display` (`mixins.py:34-39`) during a request. For a feature justified by a Selenium suite, a check (next free `E24x`) for token grammar and intra-view duplicate group ids is cheap and consistent.
- **MEDIUM:** "a request-aware hook can select distinct page/modal namespace values" — the same view serves both (`AuthorModalDetailView`, `tests/test1/app/views.py:895-900`, with `cv_modal = True`). The plan should name the mechanism the example uses to detect a modal request (the existing `X-CV-Modal` header path) so the recipe is not left to the executor.
- **LOW:** `resolve_group` at `resolvers.py:248` constructs `ResolvedGroup`; confirm kwargs so appending `id` is positional-safe (plan already says append).

**Suggestions**
- Add a system check for supplied ids; add a test that `override_settings`/`RequestContext` reuse does not leak between two `render_to_string` calls.

**Risk: MEDIUM.** Mechanism verified feasible; needs the check and the modal-detection detail.

## 06.5-05

**Summary.** Adds an extension-owned asset that instantiates Bootstrap `Tab` for owned tablists on page load and `cv:modal:loaded`, disposing on `cv:modal:unload`. The lifecycle hooks exist (`modal.js:23-24`, used by `tooltip.js:40-55`), and `register_assets` (`assets.py:56-67`) is the documented path. Two integration gaps and one design inconsistency.

**Strengths**
- Reuses the tooltip init/dispose shape including late-load handling (`tooltip.js:33-37`) and fixes its descendants-only limitation.
- Bootstrap already instantiates only *active* triggers on window load, so initializing all owned triggers via `getOrCreateInstance` is a safe superset; dispose removes the namespaced keydown handler.

**Concerns**
- **MEDIUM: bypasses `cv.adapters`.** `adapters.js:53-94` defines `modal`, `tooltip`, `collapse`, `alert`; no other shipped script references `bootstrap.` directly (grep). PROJECT.md lists the adapter layer as the theme seam and relies on it for "absence of global bootstrap". A stock asset with direct `bootstrap.Tab` becomes the first non-adapter Bootstrap dependency and lands in Phase 7's frozen inventory. The research's "no competing keyboard handler" argument does not require skipping a thin `cv.adapters.tab = {init(el), dispose(el)}`.
- **MEDIUM: registry-pinning tests not in scope.** `tests/test1/test_assets_bundles.py:10-12` pins the rendered script list; `test_pipeline_sources.py`/`test_pipeline_resolver.py` enumerate bundles for `cv_sources`. Only tests using the `asset_registry` fixture (`tests/test1/conftest.py:12-20`) see a cleared registry; the rest will now see `crud_views_object_detail`. None of these files is listed in plan 05.
- **LOW:** `CrudViewsObjectDetailConfig` (`apps.py:5-8`) has no `ready()` today; `override_settings(INSTALLED_APPS=...)` re-runs ready, so the identical-registration guard must compare against `get_registered()` rather than catch-and-ignore. The plan says this; just make sure the test exercises `apps.set_installed_apps`.

**Suggestions**
- Route through a minimal adapter entry; keep Bootstrap's keyboard behavior as is.
- Add `test_assets_bundles.py`, `test_pipeline_sources.py`, `test_pipeline_resolver.py` and the pipeline docs to read_first/files.

**Risk: MEDIUM.**

## 06.5-06

**Summary.** Small, well-bounded. `_resolve_badge_css` (`resolvers.py:94-110`) already branches callback → nonempty map → fixed color and short-circuits on `None`; the plan refactors it into one result and appends two fields. Existing precedence tests (`od/test_resolvers.py:316-374`) and the `badge_css`-before-`template` selector (`templatetags/crud_views_object_detail.py:79-85`) are correctly identified.

**Strengths**
- Appending fields preserves every kwargs-based constructor in `od/test_templatetags.py`; no positional callers found in src.
- Keeps the empty-map fallthrough and nonempty-map miss as distinct cases, which D-13 requires.
- The stock `types/default/badge.html` is untouched, so default bytes are unchanged.

**Concerns**
- **LOW:** `if prop.badge_css:` is truthiness; a resolved `""` color yields `text-bg-` (truthy). The plan preserves this; document it as existing behavior rather than contract.
- **LOW:** `_resolve_badge_label` stays a separate call; fine, but the acceptance "one result" should say color/pill/css, not label.

**Risk: LOW.**

## 06.5-07

**Summary.** Removes the alias and the obsolete template guard with a targeted before-validator. The source inventory is complete: field and after-validator (`src/crud_views/lib/view/card.py:11,31-32`), runtime branch (`src/crud_views/lib/views/card.py:51-52`), guard (`src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:126-130`), and every fixture/test/doc that spells the alias (grep listed 20 sites; all are in the plan's files or plan 09's).

**Strengths**
- Before-validator on raw mapping membership is the right tool; `CardAction` has no `extra` config, so unrelated keys stay ignored exactly as today.
- Catches the validator-bypass test (`test_card_label_mode_render.py:474-476`) and the doc-contract marker `"no_label=True"` (`test_label_mode_docs.py:217`) that would otherwise break silently.
- Fixture migration at `tests/test1/app/views.py:80` keeps rendering identical because both spellings resolve to `"icon"` at `views/card.py:49-52`.

**Concerns**
- **LOW:** between wave 1 and wave 6, `docs/migrating-1.0.md:142,157` and `CHANGELOG.md:137-140` will state that `cv_no_label` raises and `CardAction(no_label=True)` keeps working, contradicting code. Acceptable on a branch, but plan 09 must *amend* those existing Unreleased entries, not append new ones.
- **LOW:** `model_validate_json` delivers a Python dict to a before-validator in Pydantic v2 (observed 2.14), so `Mapping` membership works; add the pinned version to the summary since the plan relies on it.

**Risk: LOW.**

## 06.5-08

**Summary.** A dedicated loopback fixture and Chromium runner on port 8145, modeled on `tests/browser/conditional_formsets_server.py` and `conditional-formsets.cjs`. The case list is the right one (keyboard before click, one/two groups, page/modal isolation, stable selectors, native projection). The asset-sourcing gap is the main risk.

**Strengths**
- Separate port and files (8143/8144 are taken by `modal-focus.cjs:22` and `conditional-formsets.cjs:28`), preserving 6.4 evidence.
- Improves on the analog by discovering Playwright/Chromium rather than hardcoding a home-directory npx cache (`conditional-formsets.cjs:123-125`).
- Fails on skipped cases, page errors and unloaded assets; records versions and hashes.

**Concerns**
- **HIGH: network-bound gate.** The only stock host loads Bootstrap, FontAwesome and jQuery from CDNs (`tests/test1/app/templates/app/base.html:13-24`); there is no local copy (`node_modules/bootstrap`, `@fortawesome` absent). "Serve ... pinned Bootstrap 5.3.3" does not say from where, and the icon-font assertions depend on cdnjs. Research recommended local/recorded assets precisely so a failed download cannot masquerade as failure or success. As written the phase gate is not reproducible offline or in CI.
- **MEDIUM:** "verify stable selectors ... including translated titles" needs a language switch in the fixture (`LANGUAGE_CODE`/`Accept-Language`); not specified.
- **LOW:** the two existing runners disagree on how Node is selected (`modal-focus.cjs:11-17` reads `06-JS-RUNTIME.json`; `conditional-formsets.cjs:12`). Pick one and say which.

**Suggestions**
- Vendor the exact Bootstrap/icon files under `tests/browser/assets/` with recorded SHA-256, or declare network a precondition and fail fast with a distinct exit code.

**Risk: MEDIUM-HIGH** until asset provenance is fixed.

## 06.5-09

**Summary.** Closes with executed recipes, paired Breaking/migration entries, full three-series/JS/docs/lint gates, the existing `lifecycle` browser case (`conditional-formsets.cjs:57`, verified present) and a Phase 7 inventory. `06.5-VALIDATION.md` exists in draft, so "update" is correct.

**Strengths**
- Full-matrix honesty: three Python 3.12 series are not represented as the 3.12–3.15 matrix.
- Inventory validation test asserts names against real exports, consistent with the Phase 7 `test_stability_doc.py` direction.
- Traceability is already reconciled (`REQUIREMENTS.md:169,172` promote LATER-01/04), so research open question 5 needs no work.

**Concerns**
- **MEDIUM:** `docs/reference/object_detail_layout_packs.md` is screenshots only today (no override guidance), so 09-01 is a large authoring task compressed into a tracer; the `tokens` estimate (24k) looks low for three recipes plus full projection docs.
- **LOW:** the CHANGELOG Unreleased section already contains alias/guard statements (`CHANGELOG.md:131-140`); the plan must edit them in place to keep 1.0.0 notes self-consistent.

**Risk: LOW-MEDIUM.**

## Cross-plan assessment

**Dependency ordering** is sound: 01 → 02 → 03 → {04, 05} → 08 → 09, with 06/07 in wave 1. File overlaps are serialized (02/03 on `test_heading_level.py`; 03/04 on `presentation.py`; 04 after 06 on `resolvers.py`). Two gaps: `buttons.md` has no owner for the new heading keys, and `od/test_icons.py` plus the asset-pinning tests are affected by 02/03/05 without being listed.

**Goal coverage.** PRES-01 through PRES-06 each have a producer, a consumer migration and executed evidence. The one place the plans would not achieve the goal as written is PRES-06's real-browser gate, which depends on CDN availability.

**Scope.** No creep found. The alias and badge plans are tight; the heading registry adds roughly ten keys, which is proportionate.

**Security.** Threat registers are concrete and the escaping paths cited (`conditional_escape` in the heading node, autoescaped tag context) are the real ones. Supplied-ID grammar removes selector-injection risk.

**Overall risk: MEDIUM.** Fix before execution: (1) KEYS-04 count and `buttons.md` ownership in plan 01; (2) heading class composition in plan 01; (3) golden exclusion selector in plan 02; (4) browser asset provenance in plan 08. Recommended but not blocking: a system check for supplied group ids (plan 04), routing tab instantiation through `cv.adapters` (plan 05), and adding the registry-pinning tests to plan 05.

## OpenCode Review (grok-review)

I'll verify each plan against the current source, starting with the heading, identity, badge, and alias seams the plans depend on.
The plans match the current defects and are executable, but two mechanisms will fail their own gates unless the class-table accounting and heading class composition are made explicit.

## 06.5-01

**Summary.** The modal title is still `{% cv_heading "header" level=5 class="modal-title" %}{% cv_header_icon %} {% cv_header %}` (`src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/modal/content.html:3`), and `CvHeadingNode` always appends `h{template level}` when the rendered level changes (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:348-352`). Putting size and one host/element icon behind the existing class table is the right seam. The plan does not say whether those keys join `DEFAULT_BUTTON_CLASSES`, and that choice breaks the plan's own verify command.

**Strengths**
- Size stays independent of semantic level: the tag already reads `header_level` at render time and rejects unknown kwargs (`crud_views.py:339-376`). Adding `size=` fits that parser.
- Host versus element is already decided by `resolve_icon_name_class` (`src/crud_views/lib/icons.py:248-267`) before `render_icon` (`:231-245`). A suppressed host slot must not fall through to an inline icon; the plan says that.
- Layer-first lookup is required. `get_button_class` merges one dict (`src/crud_views/lib/button_classes.py:135-155`), so project shared cannot beat theme location unless `_heading_class` inspects layers separately. The plan does that and leaves button lookup alone.
- W362 only accepts keys in `DEFAULT_BUTTON_CLASSES` or action-mode keys (`button_classes.py:301-318`). Extending that check for optional heading keys is necessary.

**Concerns**
- **HIGH:** `tests/test1/test_button_classes.py:94-106` pins `len(EXPECTED_KEYS) == 74` and parses "The default table has 74 keys" from `docs/reference/buttons.md:147`. Task 01-02's verify runs `-k button_classes`. `docs/reference/buttons.md` is not in this plan or any later plan's `files_modified`. Adding the six size keys plus `heading:icon-host` also breaks `test_icon_host_default_slots`, which asserts exactly five `:icon-host` keys (`test_button_classes.py:310-313`). A parallel heading table avoids the count test but is not what the plan says.
- **MEDIUM:** `cv_heading` takes one `class` expression and does not interpolate `{{ }}` inside a quoted argument (`crud_views.py:349-351,371-374`). There is no class-join filter. "Attach `host_class` alongside `modal-title`" is not implementable as written. A trailing-space concat will fail `test_modal_title_is_the_header`, which requires `class == "h5 modal-title"` (`tests/test1/test_heading_level.py:161-165`). That test is not in this plan's verify, and the modal golden (`tests/test1/golden/heading_level/modal_detail.html:1`) still expects a leading space from the current template.
- **LOW:** `cv_header` is a rendering property (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:427-433`). The plan correctly says to call the icon hook once.

**Suggestions**
- Decide in the task: either add the builtin heading keys to `DEFAULT_BUTTON_CLASSES` and put `docs/reference/buttons.md` plus the 74-key sentence in `files_modified`, or keep a separate heading table and say KEYS-04 stays 74.
- Add a tag argument or escaped joiner so structural classes and `host_class` are composed without a trailing space, and assert the exact `class` string the heading-level test already uses.

**Risk Assessment.** HIGH until the key-table fork is explicit. The modal path itself is sound.

## 06.5-02

**Summary.** The two host pages and the two sibling-icon layouts are the right migration set. The test host nests the title in `h5.d-flex > h3` (`tests/test1/app/templates/app/crud_views.html:13-22`). The demo hardcodes `class="h3 mb-0"` (`examples/bootstrap5/project/templates/project/crud_views.html:17`). Split-card and striped-rows render the icon outside the heading (`layouts/split-card/group.html:8-9`, `layouts/striped-rows/group.html:4-5`). Other group layouts have no group icon.

**Strengths**
- Replacing the `h5` wrapper with a non-heading flex container fixes an invalid heading outline instead of styling around it.
- Keeping `mb-0` and moving only the size token into `size=3` matches D-01. Card and workflow headings already use `cv_heading` (`src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/card.html:6`) and should stay on the implicit size path.
- Publishing fences that actually compile is the right migration proof. `docs/reference/templates.md:47-52` is the page that still teaches the old recipe.

**Concerns**
- **MEDIUM:** `HEADINGS` excludes `ancestor-or-self::h5[contains(@class, 'd-flex')]` (`tests/test1/test_heading_level.py:26-27`). After the wrapper is removed, the page title enters every card/workflow golden unless the xpath is changed first. Those golden files are not in `files_modified`. The same verify runs `heading_level`, including `modal_detail`, whose golden is also unlisted and will change if plan 01 drops the space in `modal/content.html:3`.
- **MEDIUM:** Split-card's icon is `text-default fs-5 me-2` (`split-card/group.html:8`). The plan's builtin group override is absent, and shared `icon-inline` is `me-2`. Default split-card icons lose size and color unless that is written down as the intended diff.
- **LOW:** `tests/test1/test_manage.py:273` collects `h3, h4`. A page title that becomes `<h1 class="h3">` drops out of that list. The manage page extends the test host, so confirm it still has other `h3`/`h4` nodes before treating a failure as unrelated.

**Suggestions**
- Change the heading-level exclusion and re-read `modal_detail.html` in this plan, or add those golden paths to `files_modified`.
- State the split-card default: preserve `fs-5 text-default` in `heading-group:icon-inline`, or record the shrinkage as the intentional markup diff.

**Risk Assessment.** MEDIUM. Site inventory is accurate; the golden exclusion is the part that will fail the stated verify.

## 06.5-03

**Summary.** This is the plan that fixes the reproduced defects. Triggers mark only `forloop.first` (`layouts/tabs-vertical/object_detail.html:8-14`) while panels are always `tab-pane fade` with no `active`/`show` (`layouts/tabs-vertical/group.html:2-5`). Both sides slugify `group.title`. The accordion root is the fixed `objectDetailAccordion` (`layouts/accordion/object_detail.html:2`, `group.html:15`). `render_object_detail` renders a new context with only `groups` and `layout` (`templatetags/crud_views_object_detail.py:38`), so outer loop state cannot reach the panel. Projecting identity once and passing it into `render_group` is the correct fix.

**Strengths**
- Not mutating `ResolvedGroup` (`resolvers.py:58-64`) is required. The same groups are rendered twice by layout tests (`tests/test1/od/test_layout_packs.py:61-72`).
- `getattr(group, "id", None)` lets this plan ship before plan 04 adds the field.
- Standalone `render_group` currently emits `data-bs-parent="#objectDetailAccordion"` with no such parent (`accordion/group.html:15`). Omitting that attribute is a real fix.
- Normalizing only generated IDs in `od_accordion.html` is right. `_headings()` serializes the button, including `data-bs-target` (`tests/test1/golden/heading_level/od_accordion.html:2`). Layout smoke tests key off markers, not IDs (`test_layout_packs.py:48-67`).

**Concerns**
- **MEDIUM:** `render_group` also starts a fresh context (`crud_views_object_detail.py:54`). The parent template must pass `presentation` as a tag argument; the child template cannot see the parent's loop variable. The plan says this, but the tag signature change has to keep the current positional `group` call working for the seven packs until each template is migrated.
- **MEDIUM:** `tabs-vertical/object_detail.html` loads only `crud_views_object_detail` (`:1`). `cv_heading_icon` will live in `crud_views`. The plan never says to add `{% load crud_views %}`.
- **LOW:** `tabindex=0` on every panel is broader than the tabs pattern when the panel contains links (`test_layout_packs.py:24-30`). Plan 08 should assert where Tab lands, not only that it leaves the tablist.

**Suggestions**
- Show the exact `{% render_group %}` call and the dict `render_group` passes to the layout template.
- Add the missing `{% load crud_views %}` to the tab and accordion templates in the task action.

**Risk Assessment.** MEDIUM. The projection design matches the code. The remaining risk is template plumbing, not the ID formula.

## 06.5-04

**Summary.** Stable identity belongs on config and the view, not on slugified titles. `PropertyGroupConfig` has no `id` (`config.py:56-62`), `resolve_group` copies title/description/icon/properties only (`resolvers.py:246-253`), and the content template calls `render_object_detail` with no component id (`view_detail.content.html:3`). The collision boundary is the hard part, and the plan names it more carefully than a "use render_context" handwave.

**Strengths**
- Appending `ResolvedGroup.id` with default `None` keeps existing keyword constructors (`test_heading_level.py:45`, `test_layout_packs.py:11-33`).
- Rejecting unsafe tokens without trim/suffix matches D-07. A before-validator on the config model is the same layer as the existing property normalizers (`config.py:64-77`).
- `cv_sources` is unrelated; this plan correctly does not invent URL-fragment activation.
- Requiring Django 4.2, 5.2, and 6.0 for the scope tests is justified: the registry is Django-internal.

**Concerns**
- **MEDIUM:** `RenderContext.get`/`__setitem__` see only `dicts[-1]` (`django/template/context.py:192-200` in the 4.2 env). `Template.render` pushes a fresh frame and pops it. Storing on the current frame misses sibling includes. Storing on `dicts[0]` (the builtins dict, `context.py:32-33`) survives `Context` reuse and false-collides, which the plan forbids but an executor will do if they call `setdefault`.
- **MEDIUM:** `tpl.render({...}, request)` builds a new `Context` (`crud_views_object_detail.py:38`). Nested tags inside the layout do not share the caller's render-context. The private scope object must be an explicit template variable, and nested tags must prefer that variable over a new frame. The plan says "transport" but does not name the variable or the lookup order.
- **LOW:** `Context.__copy__` shares render-context dict objects (`context.py:157-159`). An isolated `{% include only %}` can still see a registry stored on an earlier frame object. The include/inheritance tests are what make this safe; they are specified, not optional.

**Suggestions**
- Specify: walk `context.render_context.dicts`, skip index 0, store on the earliest remaining frame only while `push_state` is active, and pass that scope object into the fresh render dict under a private name that `render_object_detail` reads before allocating a new scope.
- Validate the full projected ID set before `tpl.render`, so a colliding component raises `ImproperlyConfigured` with no partial markup from that call.

**Risk Assessment.** MEDIUM. The contract is right. A wrong frame choice will pass a single-template test and fail the include cases the plan already requires.

## 06.5-05

**Summary.** Page-load keyboard can work once the first pill has `.active` and `data-bs-toggle="pill"`, because Bootstrap constructs the active toggle on window load. Injected modal HTML cannot. `modal.js` already dispatches `cv:modal:loaded` and `cv:modal:unload` (`src/crud_views/static/crud_views/js/modal.js:23-24`), and `tooltip.js:32-55` is the right lifecycle analog. A marked initializer that only calls `Tab.getOrCreateInstance` is the correct amount of JS.

**Strengths**
- Registering through `register_assets` from `AppConfig.ready` matches the registry (`src/crud_views/lib/assets.py:56-68`). Core assets render first (`assets.py:99-101`), so `tabs.js` loads after `modal.js`.
- `crud_views_object_detail` is already installed in tests (`tests/test1/conftest.py:55`). `cv_sources()` reads the registry (`src/crud_views/lib/pipeline.py:127-141`), so a default bundle picks the file up without a core `javascript()` edit (`settings.py:302-312`).
- Not touching `modal.js` preserves the accepted focus owner. Repeat-init via `getOrCreateInstance` avoids a second keydown handler.
- The asset fixture clears `_REGISTRY` and restores it (`conftest.py:12-20`). Tests that use it will not see the new bundle unless they re-register. The plan's own asset test can register explicitly.

**Concerns**
- **MEDIUM:** `register_assets` raises on any existing key, including an identical one (`assets.py:66-67`). `ready()` runs at startup. A second `ready()` in the asset test raises unless `apps.py` compares the existing bundle and returns. The plan says that, and correctly does not swallow a conflicting path. The comparison has to use `get_registered()`, because this plan does not modify `assets.py`.
- **LOW:** Projects that pass an explicit `keys=` list to `cv_sources()` will not get `tabs.js` until they add the bundle key. Plan 09 should say that; this plan should not special-case it in core settings.
- **LOW:** Keeping `data-bs-toggle="pill"` is correct for clicks. The custom initializer must not call `show()`, or it will steal selection on re-init. The plan says that.

**Suggestions**
- In `ready()`, if `crud_views_object_detail` is already registered with the same js tuple, return; if it is registered with a different tuple, let `register_assets` raise.
- Test root-itself matching. `tooltip.js:27` only queries descendants.

**Risk Assessment.** LOW. The lifecycle is copied from a working neighbor, and the duplicate-registration edge is named.

## 06.5-06

**Summary.** Badge CSS is one function of value and config (`resolvers.py:94-110`): `None` value returns before the callback, a truthy `color_map` wins over fixed color, an empty map falls through, and `color is None` is the miss. Template choice is `badge_css` before explicit `template` (`crud_views_object_detail.py:80-86`). The stock badge already renders `prop.badge_css` and `prop.badge_label` (`types/default/badge.html:1`). Appending `badge_color` and `badge_pill` from that same result is the right change.

**Strengths**
- `resolve_property` already calls the CSS helper once (`resolvers.py:191-204`) and constructs `ResolvedProperty` by keyword. Appending fields does not break `ResolvedProperty(path=..., label=..., value=...)` in `test_templatetags.py` and `test_layout_a11y.py`.
- Preserving empty-string color (`text-bg-` because the check is `is None`, not truthiness) avoids a silent behavior change.
- A native recipe that reads `prop.badge_color` instead of parsing `text-bg-*` matches D-12 and D-14. No new `BadgeConfig` keys are needed (`config.py:18-27`).

**Concerns**
- **LOW:** `_resolve_badge_label` is a second walk (`resolvers.py:113-117`). The plan must not call `color_fn` again from a template or from a leftover `_resolve_badge_css` that does not share the private result. It says this; the callback-count test is the proof.
- **LOW:** An unresolved badge must leave `badge_color is None` and `badge_pill is False`, including a nonempty map miss. If the helper returns the requested pill flag even when color is `None`, native templates will draw a pill around ordinary values. The plan's "None/False unresolved" rule prevents that only if both fields are set from the same "no badge" branch.

**Suggestions**
- One private result object: `color`, `pill`, `css`. `resolve_property` assigns all three. Do not derive pill from `"rounded-pill" in badge_css`.

**Risk Assessment.** LOW. This is the narrowest plan and it tracks the existing branches.

## 06.5-07

**Summary.** `CardAction.no_label` is still a field (`src/crud_views/lib/view/card.py:11`), the after-validator only rejects it when it is true and combined with `label_mode` (`card.py:31-32`), and the runtime treats a truthy `no_label` as icon mode (`src/crud_views/lib/views/card.py:49-52`). `no_label=False` is accepted today (`tests/test1/test_card_action_label_mode_field.py:26-27`). Pydantic's default extra policy ignores unknown keys, so deleting the field without a before-validator would silently drop `no_label=False`. The plan's membership check is the right fix. The template guard is a separate `in ctx` check (`button_data.py:126-130`) and is fed page context via `context.flatten()` (`templatetags/crud_views.py:211`).

**Strengths**
- Presence, not truthiness, matches D-09, including `False` and `None`.
- The app fixture still constructs `CardAction(..., no_label=True)` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:79-80`). Migrating that fixture is required or the default suite cannot import views.
- Keeping singular/plural label-mode names and not setting `extra='forbid'` matches D-09 and D-15.
- `test_label_mode_docs.py:214-223` currently requires a resolution step containing `no_label=True`. The plan includes that test file.

**Concerns**
- **MEDIUM:** `docs/migrating-1.0.md:142` and `:157` still say `CardAction(no_label=True)` keeps working, and `:47` still points at the `cv_no_label` `ValueError`. Plan 07 updates the reference pages and leaves migration/CHANGELOG to plan 09. That split is declared, but any release cut between the plans would document both "removed" and "still works".
- **LOW:** `model_validate` of a non-mapping (an existing model instance) must not assume `data` is a dict. The plan's `isinstance(data, Mapping)` guard is the right shape; the task should say to return non-mappings unchanged.

**Suggestions**
- Keep one negative test that `no_label=False` raises and names `label_mode="icon"`, and one that an unrelated extra is still ignored.
- Do not grep the repo for the spelling `no_label`; migration prose and the negative test must keep it.

**Risk Assessment.** LOW. The removal is local, and the silent-ignore trap is explicitly closed.

## 06.5-08

**Summary.** Server tests cannot prove ArrowDown on a modal that was injected after `window` load. The existing runner re-execs under a pinned Node and binds a private loopback port (`tests/browser/conditional-formsets.cjs:1-32`, port 8144). A sibling runner on 8145 is the right shape. Bootstrap in the test host is the CDN build 5.3.3 (`tests/test1/app/templates/app/base.html:13-19`), which is what the keyboard claims have to load.

**Strengths**
- Keyboard-before-click is the right assertion. A click would construct a `Tab` and hide a missed `cv:modal:loaded` handler.
- Distinct page/modal component ids exercise plan 04's hook instead of a template-only fixture.
- The native case is correctly not a second tabs implementation. It checks escaped text and resolved `prop` fields.
- Explicit untested screen-reader and non-Bootstrap visual coverage matches D-16. DOM roles are not an AT certification.

**Concerns**
- **MEDIUM:** The runner is new code with a real browser, CSRF, and process cleanup. The plan describes the cases well and does not specify the server entry point beyond "existing pytest_configure/authenticated real package view patterns". `conditional_formsets_server.py` is the analog and should be the required read for auth, SQLite, and teardown, not only the cjs file. It is in `read_first` for task 08-01. That is enough if the executor follows it.
- **LOW:** CDN failure must fail the run. The plan says that. Do not stub `bootstrap.Tab` in this runner; that would duplicate the vitest plan.
- **LOW:** `tabindex=0` on panels (plan 03) can make "Tab exits the tablist" land on the panel rather than the next page control. Assert the focused element, not only `aria-selected`.

**Suggestions**
- Record the loaded Bootstrap script URL and a hash of `tabs.js` in the JSON report so a stale static file cannot pass.
- Await modal close before opener-focus assertions, as the plan says; do not assert focus in the same turn as the hide call.

**Risk Assessment.** MEDIUM. This is new harness code, but the acceptance cases are the ones the phase actually needs, and they are not inferred from mocks.

## 06.5-09

**Summary.** Copied tabs still cannot work after plan 03 unless they consume `presentation.control_id`, `panel_id`, and `selected`. The published recipes have to be executed, not grepped. Breaking entries must land next to `docs/migrating-1.0.md`, which still documents the alias as working (`migrating-1.0.md:142,157`). Phase 7 should receive names, not a stability classification. That split is right.

**Strengths**
- Fence execution with locmem overrides matches the requirement that docs are not the proof by themselves.
- Pairing each new Breaking entry with a named migration section matches the repo rule. Semantic badge fields stay additive unless an incompatible default appears.
- The public-surface handoff is scoped: heading keys, ID formula, stock marker, badge fields, alias removal. Phase 7 still owns stable/provisional/internal.
- Re-running the existing conditional lifecycle browser case is justified because plan 05 adds a document-level listener.

**Concerns**
- **MEDIUM:** Task 09-03's `<verify>` block only runs two narrow `-k` selections. The full 4.2/5.2/6.0 `pytest tests`, vitest, mkdocs, ruff, and browser commands sit in the plan-level `<verification>` section. An executor that treats `<verify>` as the gate will mark D-15 passed without those runs. The action text says to run them; the automated block does not.
- **MEDIUM:** No plan updates `docs/reference/buttons.md`. If plan 01 put heading keys in `DEFAULT_BUTTON_CLASSES`, this plan's docs selection (`heading_docs or badge_docs or label_mode_docs`) will not catch a stale "74 keys" sentence, but `test_button_classes.py:102-106` will, and it is also not in this verify block.
- **LOW:** `keys=`-filtered `cv_sources()` callers will miss `tabs.js`. The migration note should say the new bundle key, not only "redeploy static".

**Suggestions**
- Put the full-matrix commands in task 09-03's `<verify>` block, or state that a green task verify is not phase acceptance.
- Add `buttons.md` here if plan 01 grows `DEFAULT_BUTTON_CLASSES`.

**Risk Assessment.** MEDIUM. The documentation work is well scoped. The risk is claiming preservation from the narrow verify block.

## Cross-plan

Dependency order is sound. Wave 1 (01, 06, 07) does not share files. Plan 03 waits for 01 and 02 because all three touch heading behavior and 02/03 both edit `test_heading_level.py`. Plan 04 waits for 03 and 06 because 04 and 06 both edit `resolvers.py`. Plan 05 waits for the `data-cv-od-tabs` marker from 03. Plan 08 waits for the producers. Plan 09 is last.

The phase goals are covered: heading host/size (01–02), tab selection and IDs (03–05, 08), alias removal (07), badge semantics (06), and migration/handoff (02, 06, 09). Form-leaf, auth, and conditional contracts are not reopened. No plan edits `modal.js` focus logic.

The cross-plan hole is the class table. Heading keys are a public theme surface (D-17) and `buttons.md:147` plus `test_button_classes.py:102-106` are the live guard. None of the nine plans list `docs/reference/buttons.md`.

The other cross-plan hole is class composition. Plans 01–03 all tell templates to append `host_class` to an existing literal class. `cv_heading` cannot do that with one quoted argument (`crud_views.py:349-351`).

**Overall risk: MEDIUM.** The plans are grounded in the current templates and would fix the reproduced tab, ID, alias, and badge defects. They are not safe to execute unchanged: settle the `DEFAULT_BUTTON_CLASSES` versus parallel-table choice, name the heading class joiner, and name the render-scope variable before implementation.

## Consensus Summary

Two substantive, source-cited reviewers covered all nine plans. Gemini was unavailable. High-priority consensus is the heading registry/count guard; both reviewers also request explicit safe heading-class composition. The report does not infer agreement from the failed lane.

### Agreed Strengths

- The identity/selection projection addresses the actual slug-ID collisions and initially hidden panel; explicit context transport and three-Django-series scope tests match the implementation seams.
- Badge semantics derive from one callback result while preserving fallback/template precedence; exact-key alias rejection preserves unrelated input policy.
- The dependency order, executed documentation recipes, real-browser keyboard-before-click case, and Phase 7 handoff cover the phase’s intended surfaces without reopening earlier product decisions.

### Agreed Concerns

- **HIGH H1:** synchronize default heading key/host-slot tests with the documented canonical count and ensure ordered ownership of the document update.
- **MEDIUM A1:** make class composition and modal whitespace exact and testable.
- **MEDIUM A2 / LOW A3:** make host-heading exclusion precise and clarify/assert the tab-panel focus contract.

### Divergent Views

- Fable rates unspecified browser asset sourcing/preconditions **HIGH**; Grok notes the plan already fails unloaded CDN assets and treats the browser harness overall as MEDIUM. H2 remains an unresolved singleton judgment-class HIGH under the convergence rules.
- Fable raises direct Bootstrap versus a thin adapter; Grok regards the scoped stock initializer as the appropriate amount of JS. A6 requires an explicit PLAN disposition, not a mandatory adapter.
- Fable predicts existing asset-count test breakage; source inspection and Grok both identify the clearing/restoring `asset_registry` fixture. The specific claimed break is not established.

### Fable finding adjudication

The raw review above is preserved verbatim. The following distinguishes source-confirmed facts, design judgments, and concerns already represented in executable plans. No new HIGH has been downgraded on preference; no unresolved HIGH is closed merely by acknowledgement.

| ID | Severity/class | Evidence and current disposition |
|---|---|---|
| H1 | HIGH, existence-class, confirmed | `tests/test1/test_button_classes.py:95-106` requires the fixed expected-key set/count and canonical count in `docs/reference/buttons.md:147`. Plan 01 adds default keys and runs this selection, but does not own the documentation update; plan 07 owns that document only for alias prose and is in the same wave. The missing coordinated update is independently confirmed from source. PLAN must cover the registry/test/document count together and order shared document work. Do not prescribe an unmeasured future count. |
| H2 | HIGH, judgment-class | Plan 08 requires real loaded Bootstrap/icon assets but leaves source/provenance and network failure policy unspecified. Current host loads CDN assets at `tests/test1/app/templates/app/base.html:13-24`. Fable judges this a reproducibility risk. The existing plan already fails missing assets; that does not close this distinct sourcing/precondition concern. PLAN needs a concrete asset provenance/precondition strategy. A documented network precondition is an allowed route; vendoring is only the reviewer's example. |
| A1 | MEDIUM, includes overlapping LOW whitespace point | Plan 01 needs a concrete escaped host/structural-class composition and title/icon whitespace recipe, or explicit disposition explaining why existing acceptance suffices. `cv_heading` currently accepts a single class expression (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:370-373`); modal markup includes a literal separating space (`modal/content.html:3`). The review's proposed new `icon=` kwarg is non-binding. The claim that any composition is impossible is not established: conditional template composition is another route. |
| A2 | MEDIUM | Plan 02's "page card-header ancestor" exclusion needs a unique selector or explicit distinguishing rule so it cannot suppress genuine card-rows/striped-rows headings. Source: `tests/test1/test_heading_level.py:27`, both layout `group.html` card-header wrappers. |
| A3 | LOW | Plan 09's published tab-panel recipe needs an explicit rationale/qualification for panel `tabindex=0`, or a documented rejection of making it conditional on focusable content. Plan 03 requires `tabindex=0`, but does not state the theme contract qualification raised by the reviewer. |
| A4 | MEDIUM | Plan 04 should explicitly implement or defer/reject the proposed supplied-ID system checks. Existing checks (`src/crud_views_object_detail/lib/mixins.py:72-110`) and runtime validation are different paths. The user excludes optional diagnostics, so an explicit scope-based PLAN disposition is a valid route; this review does not mandate new checks. |
| A5 | MEDIUM | Plan 04/09 needs an executable page/modal identity recipe using the actual request signal, or explicit alternative. `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:58-59` identifies `X-CV-Modal: true`; `modal.js:140,177` sends it. The plan currently says request-aware hook/distinct namespaces without that concrete recipe. |
| A6 | MEDIUM | Plan 05 should explicitly resolve the thin tab-adapter suggestion against its stock-only Bootstrap ownership and no-generic-adapter constraint. `src/crud_views/static/crud_views/js/adapters.js:53-94` centralizes existing adapters; the proposed direct dependency is intentional in plan 05 but the distinction from a thin adapter has no explicit disposition. Adding an adapter is not prescribed. |
| A7 | LOW, deduplicated across plans 07/09 | Plan 09 must amend or remove contradictory current Unreleased alias/guard entries at `CHANGELOG.md:131-140`, not merely add new entries. It explicitly replaces stale migration-guide claims but does not explicitly reconcile these existing CHANGELOG claims. |
| A8 | LOW | Plan 07/09 should record the actual Pydantic version alongside removed-key validator evidence, or explicitly reject that extra metadata. The current required Python/Django/runtime report does not expressly include Pydantic. No dependency pin/change is requested. |
| A9 | MEDIUM | Plan 08's translated-title/browser acceptance needs a concrete translation fixture/activation mechanism, or an explicit disposition accepting a particular equivalent test. A language override in the fixture is one route; an Accept-Language header alone without appropriate middleware would not suffice. |
| A10 | MEDIUM, sizing advisory | Plan 09's three executed recipe families and integration documentation are questioned as too much for the low-confidence 24k estimate. Re-slice, revise the estimate, or record an explicit scope/budget rationale in PLAN; this is not a new product requirement or mandatory split. |

Already incorporated or non-actionable observations (excluded from the actionable count):

- Explicit same-level size behavior is executable in plan 01 task 01-01 (`size=3` with semantic level 3), with exact size syntax/docs in plan 02 task 02-03. No behavior suppression is implied by the review's optional alternative.
- `test_manage.py:273-274` checks a subset of four management-section headings, not an exact list including the host title. The changed host heading does not establish the predicted failure; plan 09 already runs the whole Python suite.
- Intentional split-card icon sizing/spacing changes are required by plan 02 task 02-02 and plan 09 task 09-02's intentional title/icon markup and Breaking migration documentation.
- `od/test_icons.py` need not be modified merely because it observes a moved icon; Fable itself explains its assertions remain valid. It is included by plan 09's complete suite. Read-first expansion is advisory, not a demonstrated missing behavior.
- Collision work is expressly staged: plan 03 task 03-02 carries the registry through fresh contexts and says plan 04 hardens outer-frame lifetime; plan 04 task 04-02 defines that lifetime. No conflicting second mechanism is specified.
- Preservation of existing semantic headings/description/property markup is executable in plan 03 task 03-03. The new projection does not direct removal of the panel heading.
- Appending resolved identity fields for positional compatibility is explicit in plan 04 task 04-01.
- The asserted asset count break is not established by the cited tests: pinned list/count assertions in `test_assets_bundles.py`, `test_pipeline_sources.py`, and `test_pipeline_resolver.py` use `asset_registry`, which clears/restores registry state (`tests/test1/conftest.py:11-20`). The unisolated pipeline-section test compares lazy objects, not a fixed expanded bundle count (`test_pipeline_resolver.py:40-53`). Core-only settings checks are unaffected by an extension bundle. Plan 05 already tests actual registration and plan 09 runs all suites.
- Repeat-ready/app-registry reset behavior and identical-registration-only guarding are explicit in plan 05 task 05-01; preserving conflicting-registration errors is also explicit.
- Plan 06 explicitly describes empty-string color as existing behavior in its execution boundary, and task 06-01 identifies the single result as color/pill/css while preserving badge-label behavior.
- Plan 08 already requires `CV_FOCUS_NODE` and other explicit overrides to be honored, invalid overrides to fail, and actual paths/versions to be recorded. Choosing an analog's incidental runtime lookup is not an additional required behavior.

Single-reviewer suggestions not stated as separate concerns: cache invalidation is required by plan 01 task 01-02; standalone escaped group labels are specified in plan 03's execution boundary; Context/request reuse isolation is required by plan 04 task 04-02. These do not create new outstanding items.


### Grok finding adjudication and combined dispositions

Grok's retry is a complete source-cited review; its first attempt was only an intent statement and contributes no findings or consensus weight. Gemini returned no substantive review. Thus there are two substantive reviewers in this cycle. Both have source citations and neither successful review opens with an evidence-quality discount marker.

- **H1 is corroborated.** Grok additionally identifies `test_icon_host_default_slots` (`tests/test1/test_button_classes.py:310-313`), which pins exactly five default host slots. The future shared heading host slot must be reflected in that guard as well. Grok's statement that no plan lists `docs/reference/buttons.md` is false: plan 07 lists it at line 20 and task 07-03. That does not close the real finding: plan 07 covers alias prose, not heading registry/count documentation, and runs in parallel with plan 01. H1 is about missing coordinated task coverage, not an entirely absent path.
- **A1 is corroborated** on class composition; modal spacing/golden preservation is part of the same concrete template recipe. "Not implementable" is too strong: an escaped conditional join is possible. The needed property is a specified and tested composition mechanism, not the reviewers' preferred new tag argument.
- **A2 remains actionable.** Grok notes the need to update the host-heading exclusion; the plan already directs that update, while Fable identifies the remaining ambiguity with actual content card-header wrappers. The defect is selector specificity, not total absence of an exclusion task.
- **A3 is expanded by overlapping Grok observations:** specify the expected Tab destination for panels with/without focusable content in plan 08, as well as the documented panel-tabindex contract in plan 09. One keyboard/focus contract finding, not multiple counts.
- **H2 remains a singleton judgment-class HIGH.** Grok regards the existing fail-on-CDN-error clause as good and does not rate asset sourcing HIGH. Fable's sourcing/precondition concern still counts under the convergence judgment-class rule; disagreement is recorded, not fabricated into consensus or automatic closure.

Additional actionable findings:

| ID | Severity | Evidence and PLAN change/disposition needed |
|---|---|---|
| A11 | MEDIUM | Plan 03 must explicitly load the new tag library where it calls `cv_heading_icon`, particularly `src/crud_views_object_detail/templates/crud_views_object_detail/layouts/tabs-vertical/object_detail.html:1`, currently loading only `crud_views_object_detail`. The accordion group already loads `crud_views`, so no blanket claim that every interactive template lacks the library is made. Add the exact load/import wiring and its rendering assertion, or an equivalent explicit mechanism. |
| A12 | LOW | Plan 09 needs a migration note/test or explicit disposition for `cv_sources(keys=...)`: `src/crud_views/lib/pipeline.py:82-90,132-141` filters bundles, so explicit key lists must include the new `crud_views_object_detail` bundle to receive `tabs.js`. Static redeployment alone does not change an application's explicit key list. This is the same finding repeated in Grok plans 05 and 09. |
| A13 | LOW | Plan 07 should explicitly preserve non-mapping input passthrough in the new before-validator, with an existing-instance `model_validate` regression or a reasoned disposition. The plan calls for Mapping membership and constructor/mapping/JSON tests, but does not expressly state the non-mapping branch or existing-instance case. |

Grok observations already executable or not actionable:

- Plan 03 declares appended optional tag keywords and explicitly transports matching presentation/component entries into `render_group`; it retains existing groups/group/prop and exercises all seven packs. The positional group path is not removed.
- Plan 04 task 04-02 already selects the earliest active **non-base** render-context frame, forbids Context base/request/module storage, explicitly transports a private scope into fresh contexts, and requires nested tags to reuse it. Naming the private variable is an implementation choice; the lookup/reuse invariant is already executable. No additional private public-surface name is required by the review.
- Include/inheritance, reused Context/request, nested/concurrent render tests and validation before partial output are explicit in plan 04 task 04-02.
- Plan 05 explicitly guards only identical registrations, preserves conflicts, tests app-registry reset, includes root-itself matching, and forbids show/focus changes. Using the existing `get_registered()` is a feasible implementation detail, not a separate missing invariant.
- Plan 06 explicitly derives color/pill/css once and requires unresolved defaults None/False; callback-count/fallback tests are executable.
- Migration-guide updates are explicitly deferred to dependent plan 09; no task authorizes a release between plans 07 and 09. This interim branch state is not an additional outstanding release task. The stale existing CHANGELOG entries are separately covered by A7.
- Plan 08 task 08-01 already lists `conditional_formsets_server.py` in read-first, and explicitly requires asset failure, actual browser use, source hashes/asset URLs, and completed modal close before focus assertions.
- Plan 09 task 09-03 explicitly says "Run the final verification commands below"; the plan-level verification says all three supported-series full suites are required and no nonzero/skipped/missing runtime gate can be claimed passed. Thus the full gates are executable PLAN content even though the task's fast `<verify>` block is narrower. The convergence contract expressly recognizes task action and plan verification content; this item is not invisible to execution.

The unresolved inventory is H1–H2 and A1–A13. No raw concern is removed from the audit trail. Excluded non-HIGH observations above have either concrete executable PLAN coverage or specific source evidence showing that the claimed failure is not established. None of the new actionable items is excluded merely because REVIEWS.md acknowledges it. The source/fact-drift pass contributes no additional actionable counts.

Both real CLI jobs completed: Fable and Grok, each with source-cited sections covering all nine plans. Parent-only roadmap inventory housekeeping occurred while reviewers ran; all nine plan inputs stayed byte-identical, and no requirement, decision, or acceptance contract changed. ## Claude Review (fable-review) — Cycle 2

I have verified every mechanism the nine plans rely on against the tree at `4762417`. The working tree under `src`, `tests`, `docs` and `examples` is clean at that commit, so line citations below are to committed source. I ran no tests, no browser and no application code.

# Cross-AI Plan Review, Phase 06.5, convergence cycle 2

Reviewed at commit `4762417` with repository access. Scope is pre-execution plan review. New artifacts named in the plans are treated as planned, not missing.

## 06.5-01

**Summary.** The plan now owns the whole heading-registry change, including the key-count guards and the canonical count sentence, and it specifies a concrete class-composition rule. Both cycle-1 findings against this plan are resolved by executable task content.

**Strengths**
- The composition rule reproduces today's bytes. `CvHeadingNode.render` already builds a list of a size class plus a nonempty caller class and emits `class=` only when that list is nonempty, at `src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:348-353`. Joining only nonempty size, caller and icon contributions in that order is byte-identical when the new arguments are omitted.
- The parser seam fits. Unknown kwargs raise at `crud_views.py:374-376` and `level=` must be an int literal from 1 to 6 at `:380-384`, so `size=` can reuse that validation exactly as the plan says.
- Optional location keys get validation for free. E363 iterates every merged key ending in `:icon-host` and formats it with `name_class` at `src/crud_views/lib/button_classes.py:268-278`. W362 accepts only keys in `DEFAULT_BUTTON_CLASSES` or action-mode keys at `:301-303`, which is exactly the recognition the plan extends. E361 accepts `""` as a str value at `:259-267`, so the two empty-string builtin entries are valid.
- The modal path is exact. The modal title line carries a literal space between the icon tag and the header at `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/modal/content.html:3`. The golden is `<h5 class="modal-title"> Author</h5>` at `tests/test1/golden/heading_level/modal_detail.html:1`, and the plan keeps the literal space even when the inline icon is empty. The header hook is a method at `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:422-425`, so a template expression calls it once.

**Concerns**
- LOW. The builtin `""` entries for `heading-page:icon-inline` and `heading-modal:icon-inline` live in the builtin layer. Under the plan's layer-first rule, any theme or project value for the shared `heading:icon-inline` key overrides them. That is the intended semantics, but plan 02-03's documentation must say it in one sentence so a theme author is not surprised that a shared spacing value reaches the modal title.

**Suggestions**
- Add the sentence above to the precedence paragraph published by task 02-03.

**Risk: LOW.** The plan passes its own verify as written, and the guard update is ordered before plan 07's edit of the same document.

## 06.5-02

**Summary.** The site inventory is accurate and the golden exclusion is now precise. The test host nests a heading inside a heading at `tests/test1/app/templates/app/crud_views.html:13-27`, the demo hardcodes the size class at `examples/bootstrap5/project/templates/project/crud_views.html:17`, and split-card and striped-rows render the icon as a sibling at `layouts/split-card/group.html:7-9` and `layouts/striped-rows/group.html:3-5`.

**Strengths**
- No golden captures the host wrapper. A search for `class="d-flex"` across `tests/test1/golden` returns nothing. The three `action_menus/cards_*` goldens that matched a broader search capture only the card element, as the producing test extracts the card node at `tests/test1/test_action_menus_card.py:34-37`.
- No child template overrides the host's title blocks. The only other `cv_header_icon` site in the tree is the modal template, so plan 01 and plan 02 together cover every title site.
- The test-only marker fixes A2 precisely. The current exclusion at `tests/test1/test_heading_level.py:27` keys on a Bootstrap class, while card-rows and striped-rows headings sit inside `.card-header` at `layouts/card-rows/group.html:3-4` and `layouts/striped-rows/group.html:3-5`. Excluding only an explicit attribute cannot swallow those.
- The split-card icon-style change is intentional and recorded. Tabs, accordion and striped-rows already use `me-2` at `layouts/tabs-vertical/object_detail.html:15`, `layouts/accordion/group.html:8` and `layouts/striped-rows/group.html:4`, so the shared `me-2` default changes only split-card's `text-default fs-5`.

**Concerns**
- LOW. The example project's template is not rendered by any pytest module. Task 02-01 changes it, but the only executed proof of the recipe is the documentation fence in task 02-03. A drift between the example file and the published fence would go unnoticed.

**Suggestions**
- In task 02-03, render the example template through a locmem override, or assert that its title block equals the published fence.

**Risk: LOW.**

## 06.5-03

**Summary.** The projection design matches the code. Triggers mark only the first item active at `layouts/tabs-vertical/object_detail.html:8`, panels always start `tab-pane fade` without `active` at `layouts/tabs-vertical/group.html:2`, both sides slugify the title, the accordion root is fixed at `layouts/accordion/object_detail.html:2`, and both tags render fresh contexts at `templatetags/crud_views_object_detail.py:38,54`. A11 was real and is fixed: the tab template loads only the object-detail library at `object_detail.html:1`, while the accordion group already loads `crud_views` at `accordion/group.html:1`.

**Strengths**
- The length-prefixed encoding is injective for the stated grammar, and every emitted character is selector-safe.
- Titles are typed `LazyStr` at `src/crud_views_object_detail/lib/config.py:58`. Keeping titles out of IDs avoids lazy-proxy and translation instability by construction.
- The standalone `render_group` path is preserved. `test_layout_packs.py:68-75` renders a bare group and expects the `tab-pane` marker, and `test_heading_level.py:44-47` constructs `ResolvedGroup` by keyword, so the local one-group projection and the appended optional field keep both green.
- Golden normalization is bounded to one file. Only `od_accordion.html` carries a generated target, at `tests/test1/golden/heading_level/od_accordion.html:2`.

**Concerns**
- LOW. That golden line carries the generated value in both `data-bs-target` and `aria-controls`. Task 03-03 names control, panel and parent attributes. Add `aria-controls` to the normalized set, or the golden comparison fails on the second attribute.
- LOW. `tests/test1/od/test_layout_a11y.py` holds no tab or accordion assertions today, only property-row and tooltip checks at `:11-58`. Task 03-03 describes migrating old target assertions in that file. There are none to migrate, so the new ID and selection assertions are additions.

**Suggestions**
- Name `aria-controls` explicitly in the normalization list.

**Risk: LOW-MEDIUM.** The remaining risk is template plumbing, not the formula.

## 06.5-04

**Summary.** The collision-scope mechanism is viable on Django internals, and the page/modal recipe now uses a real request signal. `cv_is_modal_request` exists at `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:57-59` and reads the header the modal script sends at `src/crud_views/static/crud_views/js/modal.js:140,177`. The test application has a modal detail view with `cv_modal = True` at `tests/test1/app/views.py:894-896`.

**Strengths**
- The "earliest non-base frame" rule holds. In the cached Django 5.2 tree, `RenderContext` reads and writes only `dicts[-1]` at `django/template/context.py:195-204`, `push_state` pushes a frame per isolated render at `:207-216`, and `BaseContext.__copy__` shallow-copies the frame list at `:40-44`, so the frame dict objects are shared by `context.new()`. `ExtendsNode` renders the parent without a push at `django/template/loader_tags.py:159`, and `IncludeNode` goes through `template.render` at `:208-210`. The outermost render therefore owns `dicts[1]`, and sibling includes, inherited blocks and `only` includes all see the same dict object.
- Threading points are correct. The mixin adds context at `src/crud_views_object_detail/lib/mixins.py:44-51`, `resolve_group` constructs by keyword at `resolvers.py:246-253`, and the content template calls the tag at `view_detail.content.html:3`.
- A4 is closed by an explicit, CONTEXT-grounded rejection, which is the allowed route. The plan keeps runtime grammar validation and render-local collision errors.

**Concerns**
- LOW. Any fresh backend render inside a page starts a new boundary, not only independent responses. The library's own formset layout helper renders through `render_to_string`, and other backend `tpl.render(dict, request)` calls behave the same. The plan 09 wording "includes and inheritance, not independent responses" should add "and not content rendered through a fresh backend render inside the page".

**Suggestions**
- Add that sentence to the boundary documentation in task 09-01 and to the migration note in 09-02.

**Risk: MEDIUM.** The mechanism is Django-internal by design. The required include, inheritance, reused-Context and three-series tests are the right guard.

## 06.5-05

**Summary.** The registration and lifecycle design is sound, but one existing JavaScript guard will fail the phase's full vitest gate as planned. This is a new HIGH.

**Strengths**
- `register_assets` raises on a duplicate key at `src/crud_views/lib/assets.py:66-67`, and `get_registered()` exists at `:71-76`, so the identical-registration guard is implementable without touching `assets.py`.
- Core renders first at `assets.py:99-101`, the object-detail app has no `ready()` yet at `src/crud_views_object_detail/apps.py:5-9`, and the app is installed in tests at `tests/test1/conftest.py:55`. This is the first shipped extension bundle; no other app calls `register_assets`.
- `tooltip.js` is the right analog, including the late-load branch at `src/crud_views/static/crud_views/js/tooltip.js:32-36` and the descendants-only limitation at `:27` the plan corrects.
- A6 is closed by an explicit disposition. The adapter module defines modal, tooltip, collapse and alert at `adapters.js:51-100`; the plan leaves it unchanged and plan 09-03 inventories the direct dependency.

**Concerns**
- HIGH, new, H3. The new `tests/js/object-detail-tabs.test.js` cannot run in the existing Node-only contract. `tests/js/helpers/load.js:8,33` resolves scripts only under `src/crud_views/static/crud_views/js`. `tests/js/runtime-contract.test.js:17-20` collects every `tests/js/**/*.test.js`, copies only that core directory into a sandbox at `:24-32`, and asserts that every collected file passes at `:69-80`. A test for `src/crud_views_object_detail/static/crud_views_object_detail/js/tabs.js` therefore fails there with a missing file. Task 05-01's own verify runs only the new file and would pass, but plan 09's required `node node_modules/vitest/vitest.mjs run` gate fails. Neither `load.js` nor `runtime-contract.test.js` is in any plan's `files_modified`.

**Suggestions**
- In task 05-01, add `tests/js/helpers/load.js` and `tests/js/runtime-contract.test.js` to files and read_first. Give the loader a package-aware path and add the extension static directory to the sandbox copy list. Keep the sandbox Node-only.

**Risk: MEDIUM-HIGH until H3 is fixed.** Everything else in the plan is grounded.

Template selection prefers `badge_css` over an explicit template at `templatetags/crud_views_object_detail.py:80-92`.

## 06.5-08

**Summary.** H2 is resolved by the allowed route. The required assets are exactly the host's, with SRI already declared: Bootstrap 5.3.3 CSS and bundle at `tests/test1/app/templates/app/base.html:14-19`, Font Awesome 6.7.2 at `:22-24`, jQuery 3.7.1 at `:27-30`, and `{% cv_js %}` at `:34` so the registered tab script loads through the registry. Ports 8143 and 8144 are taken at `modal-focus.cjs:21` and `conditional-formsets.cjs:28`; 8145 is free. The `CV_FOCUS_*` overrides exist at `conditional-formsets.cjs:12,123-125`.

D-01 to D-04 are implemented by plans 01 and 02, D-05 to D-08 by 03, 04, 05 and 08, D-09 and D-10 by 07, D-11 to D-14 by 06, D-15 to D-17 by 08 and 09.
