H1 and H2 are fully resolved in the revised plans at `4762417`. A1–A13 are closed by executable tasks or an explicit, source-backed rejection. No new unresolved findings.

## 06.5-01

**Summary.** Size and one host/element icon go through `CvHeadingNode` (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:348-376`) and the class table (`src/crud_views/lib/button_classes.py:135-155`, `:301-318`). The modal site is still `{% cv_heading "header" level=5 class="modal-title" %}{% cv_header_icon %} {% cv_header %}` (`src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/modal/content.html:3`).

**Strengths.** Implicit size matches today’s `h{level}` only when the rendered level changes (`crud_views.py:348`). `icon_class` is a second filter expression, so host classes are not concatenated inside the single `class=` argument. The modal golden’s leading space (`tests/test1/golden/heading_level/modal_detail.html:1`) is the literal separator, and the plan keeps it when `inline_html` is empty. Task 01-02 owns `EXPECTED_KEYS`, `len == 74` (`tests/test1/test_button_classes.py:94-106`), the five-host guard (`:310-313`), and the one count sentence (`docs/reference/buttons.md:147`) before that verify runs. Plan 07 `depends_on: ["06.5-01"]`.

**Concerns.** None.

**Suggestions.** None.

**Risk.** LOW.

**H1 sign-off: FULLY RESOLVED.** The guard that would fail is in the task that updates the independent expected set, host-slot count, and canonical sentence together. No future count is prescribed.

## 06.5-02

**Summary.** Host pages still nest `h5.d-flex > h3` (`tests/test1/app/templates/app/crud_views.html:13-22`) and hard-code `class="h3 mb-0"` (`examples/bootstrap5/project/templates/project/crud_views.html:17`; `docs/reference/templates.md:50`). Split-card and striped-rows put the icon outside the heading (`layouts/split-card/group.html:8-9`, `layouts/striped-rows/group.html:4-5`).

**Strengths.** `data-test-page-title` replaces the `h5.d-flex` exclusion (`tests/test1/test_heading_level.py:26-27`) without matching `card-header` wrappers (`layouts/card-rows/group.html:3-4`, `layouts/striped-rows/group.html:3-5`). Consumers use `icon_class`, not string joins. Split-card `fs-5 text-default` loss is recorded for plan 09.

**Concerns.** None.

**Suggestions.** None.

**Risk.** LOW.

## 06.5-03

**Summary.** Triggers use `forloop.first` and slugified titles (`layouts/tabs-vertical/object_detail.html:6-15`); panels are always `tab-pane fade` (`layouts/tabs-vertical/group.html:2-5`). The accordion root is fixed (`layouts/accordion/object_detail.html:2`). `render_object_detail` renders a fresh context with only `groups` and `layout` (`templatetags/crud_views_object_detail.py:38`), so the plan projects identity once and passes it in.

**Strengths.** `ResolvedGroup` stays free of render IDs (`resolvers.py:58-64`). Length-prefixed tokens stay injective. `{% load crud_views %}` is required on `tabs-vertical/object_detail.html:1`, which currently loads only `crud_views_object_detail`; `cv_icon` there is the local tag (`crud_views_object_detail.py:101-104`), not `cv_heading_icon`. Stock `tabindex=0` is an explicit choice, with both content shapes tested in plan 08.

**Concerns.** None.

**Suggestions.** None.

**Risk.** LOW.

## 06.5-04

**Summary.** Group config has no `id` (`config.py:56-62`); `resolve_group` copies title, description, icon, and properties only (`resolvers.py:248-253`). The content template calls `render_object_detail` with no component id (`view_detail.content.html:3`).

**Strengths.** Appending `id` keeps positional `ResolvedGroup(...)` calls valid. `cv_is_modal_request` is `X-CV-Modal == "true"` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:57-59`); `modal.js:140` and `:177` send that header. `AuthorModalDetailView` is `cv_modal = True` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:894-896`), so the flag alone is not the discriminator. Collision state is the earliest non-base render frame: `RenderContext.get` sees only `dicts[-1]` (Django 5.2 `django/template/context.py:200-201`). Startup checks stay out of scope; E242–E245 (`mixins.py:84-94`) are a different path.

**Concerns.** None.

**Suggestions.** None.

**Risk.** LOW. The frame rule is specified and covered on 4.2/5.2/6.0.

## 06.5-05

**Summary.** Page-load `Tab` construction does not cover HTML injected after `window` load. `modal.js:23-24` and `:94-96` already dispatch `cv:modal:loaded` / `cv:modal:unload` on the modal element. `tooltip.js:32-55` is the lifecycle analog, but only queries descendants (`:27`).

**Strengths.** `register_assets` raises on any existing key (`assets.py:66-67`); the plan compares `get_registered()` (`:71-76`) and still lets a conflicting tuple raise. Root-itself matching, no `show()`, and unmarked roots with no `bootstrap` lookup are explicit. Rejecting `cv.adapters.tab` is a stated boundary: adapters today are modal, tooltip, collapse, and alert (`adapters.js:53-94`).

**Concerns.** None.

**Suggestions.** None.

**Risk.** LOW.

## 06.5-06

**Summary.** Badge CSS is one walk: `None` returns first, then callback, nonempty map, then fixed color (`resolvers.py:94-110`). `resolve_property` calls it once (`:191-204`). Template choice is `badge_css` before explicit `template` (`crud_views_object_detail.py:80-86`).

**Strengths.** Appending `badge_color` / `badge_pill` preserves keyword and short positional `ResolvedProperty(...)` callers (`tests/test1/od/test_templatetags.py:128`). Empty-map fallthrough stays distinct from a nonempty-map miss. Unresolved defaults are `None` / `False`, so a native template cannot pill ordinary values.

**Concerns.** None.

**Suggestions.** None.

**Risk.** LOW.

## 06.5-07

**Summary.** `no_label` is still a field (`src/crud_views/lib/view/card.py:11`). The after-validator only rejects it when true beside `label_mode` (`:31-32`). Runtime treats a truthy value as icon mode (`src/crud_views/lib/views/card.py:49-52`). `no_label=True` alone still constructs (`tests/test1/test_card_action_label_mode_field.py:26-27`). Deleting the field without a before-validator would drop `no_label=False` under Pydantic’s ignore-extra default.

**Strengths.** Membership, not truthiness, matches D-09. Non-mappings return unchanged, with `model_validate` of an existing instance and a non-mapping `ValidationError` test. Plan 07 is wave 2 after plan 01 and must not rewrite the heading count. Pydantic version is recorded, not pinned.

**Concerns.** None.

**Suggestions.** None.

**Risk.** LOW.

## 06.5-08

**Summary.** Server tests cannot prove ArrowDown on tabs injected after `window` load. The host loads Bootstrap 5.3.3, Font Awesome 6.7.2, and jQuery 3.7.1 from CDNs with SRI (`tests/test1/app/templates/app/base.html:13-30`).

**Strengths.** The runner reuses those versioned URLs and integrity attributes, preflights CSS/JS/font in the browser, records SHA-256, and exits 2 on DNS/TLS/SRI/font failure without stubbing or skipping. `gettext_lazy("January")` / `"February"` resolve to `Januar` / `Februar` in Django 5.2’s `django/conf/locale/de/LC_MESSAGES/django.po:991-995`. `translation.override` wraps dispatch and `TemplateResponse.render()`. Tab from the selected trigger lands on the panel, then its link or the after-tabs control.

**Concerns.** None.

**Suggestions.** None.

**Risk.** MEDIUM. New browser process code, but the asset gate is fail-closed.

**H2 sign-off: FULLY RESOLVED.** Provenance, hosts, version, SRI reuse, font response, and the network-failure policy are executable. A documented network precondition was an allowed closure; offline vendoring was not required.

## 06.5-09

**Summary.** Copied tabs still need `presentation.control_id`, `panel_id`, and `selected`. `docs/migrating-1.0.md:142` and `:157` still say `CardAction(no_label=True)` keeps working. Unreleased changelog bullets still say the template guard raises and the constructor is unaffected (`CHANGELOG.md:131-140`).

**Strengths.** Task 09-02 edits those bullets in place and executes `cv_sources("js", keys=["crud_views", "crud_views_object_detail"])` against `pipeline.py:86-90`. Task 09-01 publishes the `cv_is_modal_request` hook and the stock panel `tabindex` qualification. The 36k estimate is an explicit budget, not a cut in acceptance. Plan-level verification still requires the three full suites, vitest, mkdocs, ruff, and both browser commands; the narrow `<verify>` block is incremental only.

**Concerns.** None.

**Suggestions.** None.

**Risk.** LOW.

## Consensus Summary — Cycle 2

### Agreed Strengths

- Both reviewers verify coordinated H1 ownership: plan 01 updates the independent key/host-slot guards and canonical count; plan 07 follows it before editing the same reference.
- Both verify H2's allowed closure: named versioned CDN/SRI assets, actual-browser preflight/provenance, bounded network prerequisites, and non-passing failure outcomes.
- Both close A1–A13, including concrete class composition, precise host-title exclusion, exact panel focus behavior, modal request discrimination, activated translations, alias input preservation, and executable migrations.
- Both find the wave graph coherent and all 17 locked decisions retained. A4 and A6 are explicit justified rejections, not silently omitted work.

### Agreed Concerns

No new concern was raised by both reviewers. Grok reports no unresolved findings. Agreement is not inferred from Gemini's unavailable lane.

### Divergent Views and HIGH gate

Fable alone raises H3; Grok's clean result does not dismiss it. Two substantive reviewers ran, neither successful section opens with a discount marker, and neither is diff-only. H3 is a judgment-class claim that the new extension test cannot satisfy the existing Node-only test contract, so it counts without corroboration. Its source premises are also independently confirmed: `tests/js/helpers/load.js:8,33` roots script reads in core; `tests/js/runtime-contract.test.js:17-20` discovers the new test automatically, `:24-32` copies only core static JS, and `:69-82` requires every discovered ordinary test to pass in that sandbox. No plan owns either harness file. No application or tests were run for this review.

The earlier H1/H2 are closed by explicit sign-off from **both** reviewers, not merely the planner's ledger. Fable's repeated MEDIUM mention of H3 under plan 09 is the same defect and is counted once. No new HIGH is suppressed by the consensus gate.

### Current executable-plan gaps

| ID | Severity | Required property and evidence | PLAN change or disposition needed (example route, not prescription) |
|---|---|---|---|
| H3 | HIGH | The new extension JS test must execute against its real asset in the existing Node-only sandbox. Source references above show that the sandbox omits the extension and the common loader only addresses core; plan 05 currently covers neither harness seam. | Make plan 05 own extension asset loading and sandbox inclusion, with the runtime-contract test in verification. A package-aware shared loader is one route; retain the Node-only boundary and do not weaken discovery/pass assertions. |
| A14 | LOW | The changed example host template must have direct verification, distinct from a separately authored docs fence. Plan 02-01 changes `examples/bootstrap5/project/templates/project/crud_views.html`, while 02-03 expressly executes published fences; no task explicitly consumes the actual example file in a test. | Require a render or equivalence check against the real example title block, or explicitly justify the existing coverage in plan 02. |
| A15 | LOW | Document exactly when collision scope is retained or lost within a page. Plan 09 describes includes/inheritance and independent responses, while generic backend renders can create fresh contexts (`src/crud_views/lib/formsets/layout.py:19,30`). Plan 04 explicitly transports scope for its own library calls, which must remain covered. | Qualify plan 09's boundary for fresh backend renders that do not carry the private scope; distinguish those from the library's explicitly transported nested renders. |
| A16 | LOW | Browser port overrides must preserve isolation from the existing fixture ports. Plan 08 declares default 8145 and honors overrides but does not reserve 8143/8144; the analog explicitly rejects its predecessor's port (`tests/browser/conditional_formsets_server.py:485-487`). | Explicitly reject reserved fixture ports (and invalid ranges) in plan 08, or record an equivalent isolation policy. |
| A17 | LOW | The real-fragment integration lane must have an explicit execution decision. Plan 09's command is ordinary vitest, whose `vitest.config.js:6` only selects `tests/js`; `package.json:12` and `vitest.integration.config.js:6` select the separate integration lane. Mentioning `CV_FRAGMENT_PYTHON` does not invoke that lane. | State the integration command with a valid selected interpreter, or explicitly justify a deferral/rejection in plan 09. |
| A18 | LOW | The Phase 7 handoff must distinguish the retained standalone `cv_header_icon` tag from the new heading contract. Plan 02-03 documents the distinction, but plan 09-03 inventories new names without expressly carrying this retained/superseded status. | Carry that distinction into the inventory task without removing the old tag or assigning final stability tiers. |

### Findings already incorporated or not separate action items

- **Shared spacing versus builtin empty slots:** plan 01's execution boundary explicitly says project/theme shared values beat builtin location values; task 02-03 publishes shared/location keys and layer-first precedence. The requested documentation property is executable already. A particular extra sentence is not an additional contract.
- **`aria-controls` normalization:** the golden does carry the target twice (`tests/test1/golden/heading_level/od_accordion.html:2`). Task 03-03 already normalizes generated control/panel/parent ID **attribute values**, retains independent real relationship tests, and requires the golden selection to pass. `aria-controls` contains the generated panel value and falls within that rule. No narrower allowlist excluding it is specified; the reviewer's preferred explicit enumeration is not a missing invariant.
- **Existing a11y file wording:** `tests/test1/od/test_layout_a11y.py:11-58` presently tests rows/tooltips. Task 03-03 also preserves those assertions, and the new identity/selection suite is explicitly created by tasks 03-01/02. The observation does not uncover missing test coverage or require removal of existing checks.
- **Cache/body hardening:** plan 08 already requires actual response-body SHA-256 and makes incomplete provenance a failing verification condition. An unreadable body cannot satisfy that requirement. A fresh context is a possible implementation choice; the broad assertion that cached response bodies are never readable is not established by this local-source review and is not used as a new invariant.
- **Selected test counts:** execution boundaries require nonzero selected cases, and plan 09 records actual command/results. Extra modules selected by `-k` do not create a coverage defect or authorize skipping the intended cases.
- **Intermediate alias documentation:** plan 07 explicitly depends on plan 09's coordinated release reconciliation; no task authorizes release between those waves. This remains non-actionable, as in cycle 1.

## Claude Review (fable-review) — Cycle 3

# Cross-AI Plan Review — Phase 06.5, cycle 3

## 06.5-01

**Summary.** The plan targets the right two producers and the only stock modal site, and its composition rule reproduces current bytes. One new concern: adding heading keys to `DEFAULT_BUTTON_CLASSES` silently enrolls headings into an existing rendering guard that no plan names.

**Strengths**
- Composition is byte-compatible. `CvHeadingNode.render` already builds a list of size class plus nonempty caller class and emits `class=` only when nonempty (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:348-353`); joining nonempty size, caller and icon contributions in that order is identical when the new arguments are omitted.
- Parser seam fits. Unknown kwargs raise and `level=` must be an int literal 1–6 (`crud_views.py:371-384`), so `size=` reuses that validation exactly.
- The modal line is exactly `{% cv_heading "header" level=5 class="modal-title" %}{% cv_header_icon %} {% cv_header %}{% endcv_heading %}` (`src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/modal/content.html:3`); the literal space the plan preserves is real, and `test_modal_title_is_the_header` expects `"h5 modal-title"` (`tests/test1/test_heading_level.py:161-165`), which the "size, caller, icon" order yields.
- Optional keys get validation for free: E363 formats every merged key ending in `:icon-host` (`src/crud_views/lib/button_classes.py:258-270`); W362 accepts only `DEFAULT_BUTTON_CLASSES` or action-mode keys (`:301-318`), which the plan extends.
- `get_button_classes()` caches the merged dict (`button_classes.py:131-139`), so the plan's instruction to read `_settings_layer()` and `_THEME_BUTTON_CLASSES` at lookup time is necessary and correct.

**Concerns**
- **MEDIUM (new, N1).** `tests/test1/test_button_classes_templates.py` builds `SENTINEL_CLASSES` from every `DEFAULT_BUTTON_CLASSES` key (`:131-133`), fetches six real pages under that override (`:232-264`) and fails on any class token that is not a sentinel, not `cv-` prefixed and not a behaviour token, on any element carrying a sentinel (`:149-159`). Once `heading:size-1..6` join the defaults, a heading that resolves a size key becomes a "styled" element. Plan 02-01 gives the test host title an explicit `size=3`, and plan 01's rule says an explicit size always resolves the key, so the host `<h1>` on all six pages carries the sentinel. Any utility class on it (plan 02's must-have says the host title is sized "without changing mb-0") is an offender. No plan lists this file; plan 02's `-k 'heading_icons or heading_level'` never selects it; plan 09 would see it only in wave 6. The same module scans `*/static/**/*.js` and `**/*.py` across every package for `btn` tokens (`:28-48`), which the new `tabs.js` and `presentation.py` must respect.

**Suggestions**
- Decide in 01-02 whether heading keys belong to the #227 button-chain guard. Either exclude `heading*` keys from `SENTINEL_CLASSES` with that rationale, or require that every explicitly sized heading on guard-fetched pages carries only table or `cv-` classes, and add the module to the verify selection of whichever plan owns the decision.

**Risk: MEDIUM** until N1 has an owner; the producer design itself is sound.

## 06.5-02

**Summary.** Site inventory is accurate and A14 is closed by a feasible test. The guard interaction from plan 01 lands here because this plan writes the sized host heading.

**Strengths**
- The test host nests `h5.d-flex > h3` with no classes on the inner heading (`tests/test1/app/templates/app/crud_views.html:14-22`); the demo hardcodes `class="h3 mb-0"` (`examples/bootstrap5/project/templates/project/crud_views.html:17`); split-card and striped-rows render the icon as a sibling (`layouts/split-card/group.html:8-9`, `layouts/striped-rows/group.html:4-5`).
- The exclusion fix is precise: today's xpath keys on `h5[contains(@class,'d-flex')]` (`tests/test1/test_heading_level.py:26-27`); an attribute marker cannot match the `card-header` wrappers of card-rows or striped-rows.
- Golden impact is bounded. `_render_group` renders with `icon=None` (`test_heading_level.py:45`), so moving the group icon inside the heading leaves `od_split-card.html` and `od_striped-rows.html` unchanged; only intentional diffs remain.
- A14 is executable: the example has exactly one `cv_heading` block (`:17-24`), Django renders `{% block %}` inside a standalone template, and the plan asserts exactly one matching fragment.

**Concerns**
- **MEDIUM (N1, shared with plan 01).** The host title with explicit `size=3` is the element the guard will style; the plan's "without changing mb-0" wording invites the executor to copy the demo's utility class onto the test host.
- **LOW.** `cv_get_view` asserts `isinstance(view, CrudView)` (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:72-75`) and both `cv_header` and the `view.cv_get_header_icon` expression need it, so the extracted-fragment test must supply a real `CrudView` instance, not a `SimpleNamespace`. Plan 01's real modal test already needs one, so this is a note, not a gap.

**Suggestions**
- State the test host title's class list explicitly in 02-01 and run `test_button_classes_templates` in its verify, or take the plan 01 route.
- Name the real view fixture the fragment test reuses.

**Risk: MEDIUM** because of N1; otherwise LOW.

## 06.5-03

**Summary.** The projection fixes the reproduced defects and A11 is closed. One consumer is unlisted: the shared fallback layout template.

**Strengths**

- Golden normalization is bounded to one file: only `od_accordion.html` carries generated targets (`tests/test1/golden/heading_level/od_accordion.html:2`); `od_tabs-vertical.html` holds only the panel `<h5 class="mb-1">`, which the plan keeps.
- `ResolvedGroup` has no render state (`src/crud_views_object_detail/lib/resolvers.py:58-63`) and `test_layout_packs.py:68-75` renders a bare group, which the local one-group projection keeps green.

**Concerns**

Under the plan, those groups get a local generated namespace instead of the component's entries, so a supplied `cv_object_detail_id` or group id never reaches a copied non-interactive layout. The file is in no plan. Decide whether the fallback passes `presentation`/`component` (one line, keeps "one projection usable by stock and copied layouts") or document that only interactive packs receive component identity.

**Suggestions**
- Add the fallback template to 03-02's files with the chosen disposition.

**Risk: LOW-MEDIUM.**

## 06.5-04

- A4 rejection cites the CONTEXT exclusion and keeps runtime validation; A5 is executable.

**Concerns**
- None new.

**Risk: MEDIUM** by nature (Django-internal frames), mitigated by the mandated three-series include/inheritance/reuse tests.

Wave order holds: 01/06, then 07 after 01, 02, 03, then 04/05, then 08, then 09. Shared `buttons.md` work is ordered. D-01–D-17 are unchanged. Form-leaf, auth, deletion, and conditional contracts are not reopened.

| ID | Verdict | Why |
|---|---|---|
| H3 | CLOSED | Plan 05 owns package-aware `load.js` and sandbox inclusion of the real extension asset; `runtime-contract.test.js` stays Node-only and must pass the new file. |
| A14 | CLOSED | Plan 02-01 compiles the title fragment read from the example file itself. |
| A15 | CLOSED | Plan 09 documents and tests untransported `render_to_string` boundaries versus transported library renders. |
| A16 | CLOSED | Plan 08 rejects 8143/8144 and invalid overrides in both entry points before bind. |
| A17 | CLOSED | Plan 09 names the integration config and the existing six-record fragment gate. |
| A18 | CLOSED | Plan 09 inventories retained `cv_header_icon` separately from the new heading contract. |

**Unresolved findings:** none.


---

## Consensus Summary — Cycle 3

### Agreed strengths and closed findings

Both reviewers independently sign off H3 as fully resolved **at plan level**: plan 05 owns package-aware loading of the real extension asset and its inclusion in the Node-only sandbox, preserves complete discovery/assertion and interpreter-negative checks, and runs the runtime-contract gate. This is explicit reviewer verification of executable coverage, not merely acceptance of the planner ledger and not a claim that future product tests already pass.

Both also close A14–A18: direct actual-example title execution, accurate transported versus untransported render boundaries, reserved/invalid-port checks at both real entry points, an explicit positive real-fragment integration command, and retained standalone cv_header_icon inventory/regression. H1/H2 and A1–A13 remain closed; their historical mentions are not counted again. The nine-plan/six-wave ordering and all 17 locked decisions remain intact.

### Agreed concerns and divergent views

No new concern is shared by both reviewers. Grok reports no unresolved findings. Fable raises N1 (MEDIUM) and N2 (LOW); neither is silently discarded because the other reviewer missed it. The newly-raised-HIGH consensus gate is not needed: there is no new or still-open HIGH. Both successful lanes have full source-citation weight, and Gemini supplies no weight.

### Current executable-plan gaps

| ID | Severity | Required property | Evidence and scope | Example route, non-binding |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| N1 | MEDIUM / WARNING | Adding heading entries to the class registry has an explicit, verified relationship to the existing button-chain rendering guard, without stripping legitimate heading utility classes or weakening button coverage. | `tests/test1/test_button_classes_templates.py:131-146` derives sentinels from every default key; `:149-159` rejects other classes on a sentinel-bearing element and its span descendants; `:250-267` applies this to real pages. Plan 01 adds heading defaults and plan 02 adds explicit size lookup, but neither names the guard's expanded scope or owns its test file. The current test host inner heading has no utility class (`tests/test1/app/templates/app/crud_views.html:15`); the demo has mb-0 (`examples/bootstrap5/project/templates/project/crud_views.html:17`). Thus Fable's specific mb-0 failure is conditional, not a proven unconditional failure. The unowned guard-scope decision is still an actionable integration risk. | Explicitly retain the guard's button-chain scope while separately verifying heading classes, or define and test compatible heading treatment; make the disposition and focused guard run visible in the owning plan. |
| N2 | LOW / WARNING | The shared fallback layout carries the enclosing component/group projection to copied group layouts, or an explicit scope decision accounts for that public behavior. | `src/crud_views_object_detail/templatetags/crud_views_object_detail.py:33-38` selects `crud_views_object_detail/object_detail.html` as fallback; that template (`:1-4`) loops raw groups and calls render_group without presentation/component. Plan 03 creates one component projection but explicitly wires only tabs/accordion templates; it gives bare render_group a fresh local projection and owns no fallback template. A copied group-only layout using this fallback can therefore lose supplied enclosing identity. | Wire fallback rendering to the same projected entries with a copied-group-only regression, or record a justified scope disposition that still honors D-05/D-08 and the shared projection contract. |

The current plans do not incorporate or explicitly disposition either new finding. N1 and N2 are counted once each; Fable's repeated N1 mention under plan 02 is not a third issue. A fix must preserve the invariant; the examples do not prescribe file allocation or implementation.

### Observations that are not additional action items

- The extracted example test needs a real CrudView. Plan 02-01 already requires the same **real** heading/icon producer fixtures, and Fable itself labels this a note rather than a gap.
- The existing no-button-literal source scanner also covers new JS/Python files. No task introduces a btn literal or requires a conflicting selector; the plan's full preserved suite still runs the scanner. This adds no independent finding beyond the N1 integration decision.
- A cross-reference from `docs/reference/javascript.md` to the initializer is a documentation organization suggestion. Plan 09-01 already documents its stock marker/initializer/lifecycle, and 09-03 inventories the public initializer, consumers and migration references. A second particular documentation location is not a missing requirement; no extra actionable count is assigned.

```yaml
issues:
  - plan: "06.5-01/06.5-02"
    dimension: "cross_plan_integration"
    severity: "warning"
    review_severity: "MEDIUM"
    required_property: "Heading registry additions have an explicit verified relationship to the button-chain sentinel guard while heading utilities and button coverage are preserved."
    description: "N1: test_button_classes_templates.py:131-159 includes all default keys in its sentinel-only rule; plans 01/02 add heading keys and explicit sizing without a guard-scope disposition or focused integration check. A utility-class failure is conditional, not established as inevitable."
    fix_hint: "Define the guard's intended scope in the owning plan and verify it without weakening the established button-chain assertions."
  - plan: "06.5-03"
    dimension: "key_links_planned"
    severity: "warning"
    review_severity: "LOW"
    required_property: "Fallback layout rendering preserves the enclosing component/group identity projection for copied group layouts or records a justified scope disposition."
    description: "N2: object_detail.html:1-4 calls render_group without presentation/component and is the fallback selected at templatetags/crud_views_object_detail.py:33-38; plan 03 wires only interactive template consumers and creates local identity for bare groups."
    fix_hint: "Wire and test the fallback consumer against the same projection, preserving supplied stable identity."
```

## Claude Review (fable-review) — Cycle 4

# Cross-AI Plan Review — Phase 06.5, convergence cycle 4

Reviewed at HEAD `91f93d4` with repository access. The product tree under `src`, `tests`, `docs` and `examples` is clean at that commit, so every citation below is to committed source. Read-only: no tests, browser or application code were run. Planned new artifacts are treated as planned, not missing. One evidence limit: the sandbox blocked reading Django's German catalog under `.nox`, so the `Januar`/`Februar` fact relied on by plan 08 is carried from cycle 3, not re-read here.

## 06.5-01

**Summary.** The producer design is unchanged and still matches the two seams it modifies. The cycle-3 N1 revision assigns the guard-scope decision to plan 02 and keeps plan 01's own registry/count guards complete. That split is consistent with the wave order and with what the guard actually renders in wave 1.

**Strengths**
- Composition reproduces current bytes. `CvHeadingNode.render` builds a list of the implicit size class plus a nonempty caller class and emits `class=` only when nonempty (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:348-353`). "Size, caller, icon" joined only when nonempty is identical when the new arguments are omitted, and yields `"h5 modal-title"` for `test_modal_title_is_the_header` (`tests/test1/test_heading_level.py:161-165`).
- Parser seam fits. Unknown kwargs raise at `crud_views.py:374-376`; `level=` must be an int literal 1..6 (`:380-384`), so `size=` can reuse that validation.
- Registry mechanics are as the plan assumes: settings layer read uncached (`src/crud_views/lib/button_classes.py:131-132`), merged cache (`:135-141`), presence-based mode fallback (`:150-155`), E361 accepting `""` (`:259-267`), E363 formatting every merged `:icon-host` key (`:268-278`), W362 accepting only defaults or action-mode keys (`:301-318`). The two builtin `""` inline entries pass E361.
- The modal line and golden are exactly as described: `modal/content.html:3` has the literal space; `tests/test1/golden/heading_level/modal_detail.html:1` is `<h5 class="modal-title"> Author</h5>`.
- Wave-1 guard run is sound. Task 01-02's `-k button_classes` selects `test_button_classes_templates.py`. In wave 1 the sentinel set will include the new heading keys (`:132`), but no guard page can render one: the test settings set no `CRUD_VIEWS_HEADER_LEVEL` (`tests/test1/conftest.py`, no such key), so implicit sizes never resolve, and no explicit `size=` exists until plan 02. The guard pages (`:232-239`) do not fetch the modal.

**Concerns**
- LOW. The plan's "derive the resulting counts at execution" is right, but the summary should state the derived numbers for the reader of `buttons.md:147`: 74 existing keys plus six size keys, two shared icon slots and two builtin empty location-inline entries, and six `:icon-host` slots instead of five (`tests/test1/test_button_classes.py:310-313`).

**Suggestions**
- Record the final key and host-slot counts in the 01 SUMMARY so plan 07 (which edits the same document afterwards) and Phase 7 can cite them.

**Risk: LOW.**

## 06.5-02

**Summary.** The N1 closure lives here and it is precise. The partition rule excludes exactly the four heading namespaces, nothing else exists under those names today, and the positive leak-detection regression proves the button-chain guard still bites.

**Strengths**
- The guard is exactly as the plan describes: sentinels from every default key (`tests/test1/test_button_classes_templates.py:131-133`), styled-element selection (`:144-146`), offender rule covering span descendants (`:149-159`), leaf and six-page sweep under the override (`:250-264`), positive styled counts (`:266-267`). A grep of `src` for `heading:`/`heading-` keys returns nothing, so the "location segment before the first colon" rule removes only plan 01's additions.
- The scoping does not weaken the existing heading that is already guarded by a button key: `tags/card.html:6` sizes the card title through `card:title`, which stays a sentinel, and under the guard pages' `header_level=None` it carries only that class.
- Site inventory confirmed: the test host nests `h5.d-flex > h3` (`tests/test1/app/templates/app/crud_views.html:13-27`); the demo hardcodes `class="h3 mb-0"` (`examples/bootstrap5/project/templates/project/crud_views.html:17`); split-card and striped-rows render the icon as a sibling (`layouts/split-card/group.html:8-9`, `layouts/striped-rows/group.html:4-5`); `docs/reference/templates.md:47-55` still teaches the old recipe.
- A14 holds: the example has one `cv_heading` block (`:17-24`) and `cv_get_view` demands a real `CrudView` (`crud_views.py:72-75`), which the plan's real-fixture instruction satisfies.
- The exclusion replacement is unambiguous: today's xpath keys on a Bootstrap class (`tests/test1/test_heading_level.py:26-27`); a test-only attribute cannot match the `card-header` wrappers in `card-rows/group.html:3` or `striped-rows/group.html:3`.

**Concerns**
- LOW. `test_every_literal_slot_in_templates_is_a_default_key` (`test_button_classes_templates.py:51-57`) fails for any template literal naming an optional non-default key such as `heading-page:icon-host`. The plans route all heading lookups through `_heading_class`/`cv_heading_icon`, so this is a constraint to keep, not a defect. State it in 02-03's recipe so a copied template does not use a literal for an optional key.

**Suggestions**
- Add one sentence to 02-03: optional location keys are addressable through the tag, never through a template literal.

**Risk: LOW.**

## 06.5-03

**Summary.** The projection fixes the reproduced defects, and the N2 closure is now a real production change in task 03-03 with the fallback as a fifth owned file. The mechanism is correct for all five group-only packs.

**Strengths**
- Defects confirmed on source: trigger-only first selection and slug IDs (`layouts/tabs-vertical/object_detail.html:7-14`), panels always `tab-pane fade` (`layouts/tabs-vertical/group.html:2-5`), fixed `objectDetailAccordion` root and parent (`layouts/accordion/object_detail.html:2`, `group.html:15`), fresh contexts in both tags (`templatetags/crud_views_object_detail.py:38,54`).
- N2 premise confirmed: the shared fallback loops raw `groups` and calls `render_group` with no projection (`crud_views_object_detail/object_detail.html:1-4`), is the second `select_template` candidate (`:32-37`), and is the only `object_detail.html` for split-card, card-rows, table-inline, list-group-3col and striped-rows (the layouts tree lists `object_detail.html` only under accordion and tabs-vertical).
- The migration is one-directional and safe: 03-01 builds the projection once in `render_object_detail`, 03-03 iterates `component.groups` and passes `presentation`/`component`, and `render_group` with a supplied entry must not allocate. The standalone `render_group` paths still exercised by `test_layout_packs.py:69-75`, `test_heading_level.py:44-47` and `od/test_icons.py:11-15` keep their local projection.
- A11 holds: `tabs-vertical/object_detail.html:1` loads only the object-detail library, whose `cv_icon` is local (`crud_views_object_detail.py:101-104`); the accordion group already loads `crud_views` (`accordion/group.html:1`).
- Golden impact is bounded to `od_accordion.html:2`, which carries the generated value in `data-bs-target` and `aria-controls`; the normalization rule covers attribute values, not names.
- The locmem override pattern has precedent in `tests/test1/test_heading_tag.py`, `test_crispy_buttons.py` and others, so overriding only `group.html` while the fallback stays on disk is feasible.

**Concerns**
- LOW. `od/test_icons.py:40-41` asserts `'<i class="truck'`; once split-card/striped-rows and the tab button render icons through the shared inline slot (`me-2`), `render_icon("truck", css="me-2")` still yields that prefix (`src/crud_views/lib/icons.py:231-245`). This is fine, but the file is owned by no plan and only plan 09's full suite exercises it. Name it in the 03 summary as an observed-green regression.

**Suggestions**
- None beyond the summary note.

**Risk: LOW-MEDIUM** (template plumbing across three tasks; the formula and fallback wiring are sound).

## 06.5-04

**Summary.** Stable identity threading and the collision boundary are viable on verified Django internals. The N2 extension in 04-01 covers the supplied-group case through the real view and the unchanged fallback.

**Strengths**
- Threading points verified: `mixins.py:44-51` adds context; `view_detail.content.html:3` passes nothing today; `PropertyGroupConfig` has no `id` (`config.py:56-62`); `parse_property_display` is `PropertyGroupConfig(**group)` (`:88-90`); `resolve_group` constructs by keyword (`resolvers.py:248-253`). Appending `id` is additive.
- The modal recipe is real: `cv_is_modal_request` reads `X-CV-Modal == "true"` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:57-59`), sent by `modal.js:140,177`; `AuthorModalDetailView` sets `cv_modal = True` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:893-896`), so the flag is not the discriminator.
- The frame rule holds on the Django 5.2 tree: `RenderContext.get` reads `dicts[-1]` (`django/template/context.py:195-200`), `push_state` pushes one frame per isolated render (`:207-216`), `BaseContext.__copy__` shallow-copies the frame list (`:40-44`) so includes and `only` includes share the frame objects, `ExtendsNode` renders the parent with `isolated_context=False` (`loader_tags.py:158-159`), and the backend `tpl.render(dict, request)` builds a new `Context` via `make_context` (`backends/django.py:102-103`). The outer render's `dicts[1]` is therefore the right owner, and fresh backend renders need the explicit transport the plan specifies.
- 04-01's test uses the same shipped fallback with copied `group.html`, resolved explicit IDs and the request-aware hook; this is the supplied-identity half of N2.

**Concerns**
- LOW. The only other `render_object_detail` consumer in the tree is `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/view_detail.content.html:1-3`, which loads a tag library named `object_detail` that does not exist (`src/*/templatetags/` contains only `crud_views`, `crud_views_formsets`, `crud_views_object_detail`). Nothing references it; it last changed in the `src/` move commit. It cannot carry `object_detail_id` and would raise `TemplateSyntaxError` if ever rendered. Not a plan defect and outside the phase's cleanup exclusion, but plan 04/09 documentation must name the object-detail app's content template as the only supported threading point. See open question below.

**Suggestions**
- In 04-01's action, state that `crud_views_object_detail/view_detail.content.html` is the sole stock path for `object_detail_id`.

**Risk: MEDIUM** by nature (Django-internal frames), mitigated by the mandatory three-series include/inheritance/reuse tests.

## 06.5-05

**Summary.** H3 remains fully resolved; the asset registration and lifecycle hooks are as described. No regression.

**Strengths**
- `register_assets` raises on any existing key (`src/crud_views/lib/assets.py:66-67`), `get_registered` exists (`:71-76`), core renders first (`:99-101`), the object-detail app has no `ready()` (`src/crud_views_object_detail/apps.py:5-9`), and the test host emits `{% cv_js %}` (`tests/test1/app/templates/app/base.html:34`).
- Lifecycle events are exactly `cv:modal:loaded`/`cv:modal:unload` (`modal.js:23-24`); the tooltip analog scans descendants only (`tooltip.js:27`), which the plan corrects.

The A4 and A6 rejections cite CONTEXT exclusions and keep existing adapters; no decision is reopened and no scope is simplified away.

**Prior closures.** H1, H2, H3 and A1–A18 remain closed. I found no regression in the cycle-4 revisions; the N1 and N2 edits touch only `test_button_classes_templates.py`, the fallback template and the identity tests.

| ID | Severity | Verdict | Independent evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| N1 | MEDIUM | **FULLY RESOLVED at plan level** | Guard derives sentinels from every default key and flags non-sentinel classes on sentinel-bearing elements (`tests/test1/test_button_classes_templates.py:131-159,250-267`). No existing key uses a `heading*` location, so 02-01's four-namespace exclusion removes exactly plan 01's additions. `card:title` keeps guarding the card heading (`tags/card.html:6`). Plan 01's wave-1 guard run passes because the test settings set no header level and no explicit `size=` exists before plan 02. The added positive leak regression keeps button/span detection proven. |
| N2 | LOW | **FULLY RESOLVED at plan level** | Fallback confirmed at `crud_views_object_detail/object_detail.html:1-4`, selected at `templatetags/crud_views_object_detail.py:32-37`, and the sole `object_detail.html` for the five group-only packs. 03-03 owns the file, passes `entry.group`/`presentation`/`component`, forbids re-allocation, and tests all five packs through the real fallback with a supplied component. 04-01 extends to resolved explicit group IDs and the request-aware hook via the real view. No HIGH or MEDIUM finding is open.

## Consensus Summary — Cycle 4

### Agreed Strengths and explicit closure

Both reviewers independently sign off **N1 and N2 FULLY RESOLVED at plan level**, confirm all nine plans, retain H1/H2/H3 and A1–A18 as closed, and find no regression in D-01–D-17. This is reviewer confirmation of executable PLAN coverage, not an acknowledgement-only closure or a claim that future implementation passes.

| ID | Previous severity | Independent closure evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| N1 | MEDIUM | Fable and Grok trace the registry-wide sentinel enrollment at `tests/test1/test_button_classes_templates.py:131-159,250-267`. Task 02-01 partitions only the four heading namespaces, retains every non-heading key and existing page/leaf/span/scanner check, and demands both legitimate heading-utility acceptance and injected button/span leak detection. Plan 01 coordinates ownership and retains complete registry/host-slot guards. Both check wave-1 timing: the existing guard URLs omit modal content, and implicit sizing does not resolve under default header_level=None. | FULLY RESOLVED |
| N2 | LOW | Both verify the real shared fallback at `src/crud_views_object_detail/templates/crud_views_object_detail/object_detail.html:1-4`, selected by `templatetags/crud_views_object_detail.py:32-38` for the five group-only packs. Task 03-03 passes original entry/component without allocating again and tests all five copied groups through the shipped fallback. Task 04-01 adds resolved explicit group IDs and request-aware real-view page/modal repeats, translation and reorder. | FULLY RESOLVED |

Both reviewers confirm serialization of shared files and the unchanged six-wave order: 01/06 → 02/07 → 03 → 04/05 → 08 → 09. There are nine plans and 22 tasks. Both retain the required real-browser, supported-series, Node-only and positive real-fragment integration gates; neither accepts the baseline as new acceptance.

### Agreed Concerns

None remaining. No Gemini agreement or approval is inferred.

### Divergent notes and actionability decisions

Fable records LOW observations that Grok does not raise. They remain visible verbatim above. None establishes a missing Phase 06.5 execution invariant:

- **Final registry counts in a SUMMARY:** 01-02 already requires deriving the complete independent key/host sets and updating the single documented count; its output requires actual changed contracts/results. Adding the same numbers at a preferred summary location is presentation advice, not missing registry/count coverage. No unmeasured total replaces the planned execution measurement.
- **Optional heading keys in literal template lookups:** 01 defines `_heading_class`/`cv_heading_icon` lookup, and 02-03 executes recipes using that public tag; 02-01 explicitly preserves the whole-registry literal-slot assertion and source scanner. Fable itself calls this a constraint, not a defect. The plan does not direct an unsupported literal lookup, and a preferred extra sentence does not add a missing mechanism.
- **Existing icon regression timing:** 09's mandatory full suites cover `tests/test1/od/test_icons.py`, and 03 already preserves all layout/heading checks. Source at `src/crud_views/lib/icons.py:231-245` supports the retained prefix; asking to name the observed-green result in 03's summary is not an uncovered regression gate.
- **Unused core content template / Phase 7 inventory:** Direct source confirms `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/view_detail.content.html:1-4` contains the old tag-library spelling, while `src/crud_views_object_detail/lib/mixins.py:20` selects `crud_views_object_detail/view_detail.content.html`. The current source references and existing render test (`tests/test1/od/test_object_detail_view.py:123`) use the latter. Plan 04 already names that exact extension path in frontmatter, key links and 04-01 files/read-first (`06.5-04-PLAN.md:11,36,85-88`); there is no ambiguous production threading target to repair. Plan 09-03 explicitly inventories the phase's new inputs/fields with producer and consumer references and leaves final stable/provisional/internal classification to Phase 7 (`06.5-09-PLAN.md:69,113-114`). The unused legacy file is neither introduced nor claimed supported by these plans. Fable explicitly calls it pre-existing, unused, not a product regression and not a plan defect. A requested extra historical-file entry is advisory scope for the later inventory, not an executable gap in this phase; no stale file is silently deleted, promoted or certified here.
- **German month evidence limit:** Fable could not re-read its cached catalog, and clearly labels that limitation. Supplemental read-only inspection this cycle confirms `django/conf/locale/de/LC_MESSAGES/django.po:991-995` under the named Django 5.2 cache contains January/Januar and February/Februar. This does not claim actual rendered acceptance: 08-02 already requires activated translation, observed changed labels, stable IDs and recorded case outcomes.
- **New JS source scanner:** 02-01 explicitly retains the scanner over future `tabs.js` and `presentation.py`. Plan 05's selectors do not require forbidden button-style tokens. This constraint is already executable, not an unincorporated concern.

### HIGH consensus gate and current result

Two substantive independent reviewers ran. Neither successful review opens with a source-quality discount marker; neither lane is diff-only. No newly raised HIGH exists, so no finding is suppressed by the singleton existence/judgment gate, and fail-open discount handling is unnecessary. There is no remaining actionable MEDIUM/LOW item invisible to execution. N1/N2 close through both reviewers' explicit sign-off; the LOW notes above are already covered by executable content or do not assert a phase requirement defect. No plan revision is required by this cycle. Final implementation and all planned verification remain outstanding.

### Independent direct-source checks

The saved `independent-source-checks.json` records source hashes and exact excerpts. No application or tests were run for this review.

- `tests/test1/test_button_classes_templates.py:131-159,250-267`: registry-wide sentinels and real-page/button/span traversal substantiate N1's original premise. Revised 02-01 now explicitly preserves that coverage while partitioning only four heading namespaces, with positive styled cases and injected leak failures.
- `src/crud_views_object_detail/templates/crud_views_object_detail/object_detail.html:1-4` and `templatetags/crud_views_object_detail.py:29-54`: shared fallback selection and fresh group contexts substantiate N2. Revised 03-03 wires the original projection through the real fallback; 04-01 verifies resolved group IDs and request-aware view tokens through that same path.
- `examples/bootstrap5/project/templates/project/crud_views.html:17-24`: the unique real example heading carries mb-0; 02-01 consumes its actual source rather than a separate documentation recipe.
- `tests/js/helpers/load.js:8,32-41` and `tests/js/runtime-contract.test.js:17-40,63-102`: the H3 loader/sandbox seams remain covered by 05-01/02 without weakening Node-only discovery/pass assertions.
- `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:57-59` and `vitest.integration.config.js:6`: real modal request discriminator and separate positive integration lane remain explicitly covered.
